Global Research
Fast Take
ChipMOS Technologies
Headline
ChipMOS posted Aug EPS of NT$0.62, accounting for 49%/46% of UBSe/street
Q3 EPS.
Our Take
Better Aug EPS should be driven by OpM strength rather than non-op. With Aug
sales already reported tracking 69%/68% of UBS/street for 10+% QoQ growth,
and GM likely improving sequentially in Q3, we believe Q3 EPS is tracking above
expectations.

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on final JMTBA orders, which we believe
could be among the catalysts for Airtac's and Hiwin's share
prices.
Per the Japan Machine Tool Builders' Association (JMTBA), headline orders grew
2% MoM and 65% YoY in August 2026 (vs. -5% MoM/+50% YoY in July 2026), to
¥198bn.
• Domestic orders  -3% MoM/+62% YoY (vs.-8% MoM/+50% YoY     in July), to
¥52bn
• Overseas orders  +5% MoM/+66% YoY (vs. -4% MoM/+51% YoY   in July), to

¥146bn
• By region:
° China: -2% MoM/+62% YoY, to ¥47bn
° Europe: -12% MoM/+44% YoY, to ¥20bn
° North America: +24% MoM/+72% YoY, to ¥50bn
Our view:
• We think the solid YoY growth for overseas orders continues to point to an
upcycle scenario, despite some seasonal volatility  with MoM growth in some
regions,
• On top of overall segment recovery within automation:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should benefit from its own share gains and
incremental contributions from linear motion components. Valuation

appears undemanding at 17x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show margin expansion in the coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside as the stock now trades at 29x 2027e P/E
(vs. peak cycle valuation of 35-40x).
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.

Telecom & Networking Equipment  | North America
Potential FCC Rules on Optical
Transceivers More Likely to
Come in at 3.2T
Recent DC meetings highlight 3.2T as the most likely generation
to see restrictions on China-based optical transceivers. This
echoes conversations with LITE during the Q noting that any
restrictions would need to be phased in. See potential rules
keeping laser market tight, a pos for LITE/COHR.
Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC

Morgan Stanley MUFG Securities Co., Ltd.+
Yu Shirakawa
Equity Analyst
Yu.Shirakawa@morganstanleymufg.com                                                +81 3 6836-5432
Telecom & Networking Equipment
North America
Industry View In-Line
What Happened: Morgan Stanley strategists recently  met with a senior Washington
telecom, FCC and export-control legal team with direct engagement across the
White House and FCC. Discussion focused on the Administration’s efforts to reduce

reliance on Chinese technology, with the FCC Covered List emerging as the more
likely near-term mechanism for optical transceivers (a note we made in our AI Safety
DC trip wrap-up last week). Policy discussions suggest 3.2T transceivers would likely
be where restrictions would be phased in, potentially as soon as October, with
industry recently asked to demonstrate credible U.S. or friend-shoring plans before
3.2T becomes broadly deployed. Our conversations noted that discussions are also

indicating Chinese-built transceivers could still be importable, even at 3.2T, as long
as 65% of the BOM comes from US-based companies (DSP / laser would get you
pretty close). Initial 3.2T volumes are not expected to ramp in volume until 2029,
limiting the near-term revenue impact of a restriction even if action comes sooner.
We would see these rules as having the potential to avoid creating a constraint
on hyperscalers' ability to build today, but keeping the  laser market tighter given

laser would largely need to be from a US-based vendor to meet a 65% value
threshold, a positive for LITE/COHR.
How this echoes conversation with LITE earlier in the quarter: We hosted LITE at
Nasdaq Asia in late August, where we asked the company about potential FCC rules.
At the time they noted that the FCC had been in contact with them about potential
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Recent DC trip by our strategist   team gave a greater idea of what potential FCC
rules on optical transceivers could look like.
Potential restrictions, likely to come in at 3.2T, limit Chinese-based transceivers,
with a potential workaround if US-based content above 65% of the BOM.
Solution seemingly a win-win for LITE/COHR, with laser market likely to stay

tight, but not having to take on lower module assembly business.
Also, does not create bottlenecks for hyperscalers.
Assuring InP substrate delivery will be key to look at going forward.
October 1, 2026 04:09 AM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
rules, with the company noting their feedback was that the rules would need to be
phased in over time to prevent material supply/demand dislocation in the market
with 60-65% of transceivers coming from China-based transceiver vendors. They
also noted that pure transceiver volumes alone, at current pricing structures, would
not be the most attractive business for them to bring on. These rules, which are
both phased in and don't limit Chinese assemblers, as long as enough content is

US-based, would seemingly address both of these concerns.
Our Thoughts:
1. 3.2T looks like the likely policy pressure point, but the near-term impact
should be limited - Administration efforts appear focused on reshaping the
supply chain before 3.2T reaches volume rather than disrupting the existing
800G and 1.6T base. With the 3.2T ramp not expected to begin until 2028
and broader adoption closer to 2029, an October Covered List action would
provide roughly two years before meaningful volumes enter the market.

Hyperscalers are already working on plans intended to demonstrate greater
U.S. or friend-shored manufacturing, suggesting the policy objective is
increasingly about determining where the next generation is built rather
than immediately displacing current Chinese supply.
2. Laser pricing looks like the stronger read-through versus transceiver
share - A Covered List framework does not necessarily require Chinese
module vendors to lose significant share if they can increase the U.S.

content of the underlying BOM. Combining a U.S. DSP from Marvell with
laser content from LITE/COHR could potentially move a Chinese-built
transceiver to the 65% U.S.-value threshold discussed in our strategists
team policy meeting. Such an outcome would leave Innolight and Eoptolink
(both covered by Andy Meng) with less flexibility to substitute toward
China-based lasers while allowing them to remain relevant at the module
level, reducing the risk from China-based laser makers. We therefore see a

stronger read-through for high-speed laser pricing and LITE/COHR
component share than for a wholesale redistribution of transceiver share.
3. Replacement capacity not ready; nor is it the most attractive opportunity
- As noted above, LITE noted that assembly of transceivers (mid-30's to low-
40s gross margin product), is not the most attractive opportunity for US-
based optical companies (vs. EMLs and lasers well north of 50% steady
state and approaching 75% today). While 3.2T is not yet in production, we

use AAOI's current 800G and 1.6T capacity ramp as an illustration of the
scale of US supply available to absorb a potential future demand shift. AAOI
(not covered), the only material US-based production of transceivers, is
currently ramping capacity. AAOI exited Q2 with manufacturing capacity
approaching 200,000 800G and 1.6T units per month and expects to reach
~650,000 units per month by year-end 2026, while management continues
to expect demand to outpace production through mid-2027. AAOI’s YE26

capacity annualizes to ~12M 800G/1.6T units, versus ~101M units
combined for Innolight and Eoptolink based on annualized 1H capacity
(44.3M and 56.7M, respectively), or >8x AAOI. Based on AAOI’s year-end
capacity plans and our Internet team’s hyperscaler capex work, we estimate
AAOI could support less than 4 GW of hyperscaler capacity even before

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
factoring in manufacturing utilization. Replacing a meaningful portion of
Innolight and Eoptolink’s supply would therefore require a substantial multi-
year capacity build, with limited ability for existing U.S. suppliers to absorb
those volumes in the near term.
4. InP supply and sourcing outside of China will still be areas to watch -
Potential FCC rules could create a win-win for LITE/COHR by reducing the
risk of China-based laser makers flooding the market, without forcing them

to build significant transceiver module capacity to absorb lower-margin
assembly business. Were China to view the rules as too one-sided, it could
respond with tighter restrictions on InP substrate exports, a key bottleneck
to laser capacity ramping. At the same time, sourcing outside of China could
broaden the beneficiary set beyond U.S. suppliers, supporting InP substrate
demand at JX Advanced Metals (5016 JP), while providing a tailwind for
Sumitomo Electric’s (5802 JP) CWLD products and Furukawa Electric’s

(5801 JP) DFB lasers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its August results (for pre-tax profit and net profit) after 
market hours on 2 October mainly due to recent volatile share price movements. Net profit 
in August 2026 came in at TWD1,092m (EPS: TWD1.69), up 665% YoY, with pre-tax profit 
at TWD1,486m (pre-tax margin at 27.8%). The August pre-tax margin of 27.8% is close to 
our current forecast (28.5%/32.9% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). As we expect limited impact from 
non-ops and on opex in August, we infer the gross margin in August should have reached 
to c.30-31% (vs. our forecast c.30.3/34.4% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). This echoes our previous 
notes (Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand); we expect NYPCB’s 
gross margin will recover to c.30% after Kunshan’s yield rate improvement in August. As 
such, we believe the August results to be largely on track with market expectations. 
However, we believe the share price upside will come from BT substrate, where we should 
see better progress in its implementing price hikes now (vs. limited visibility earlier in 
September). We expect NYPCB to raise ASP of BT substrates by single digits QoQ in 
4Q26 and +20% QoQ in 1Q27. This is aligned with our previous comment (Clarification on 
“no intention to hike price from 4Q26”) that we expect BT substrate prices will accelerate 
again in 2027 despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E. We view NYPCB’s 
August results announcement as a near-term price catalyst. We have a Buy rating (1) on 
NYPCB with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand 
for switches/PCs/ smartphones.

Hon Hai (2317.TW): Sep beat: revenues +38% YoY; New AI servers, CPO 
switch and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26; Buy (on CL)
5 October 2026 | 11:23PM HKT
Hon Hai’s Sep revenues were +38% YoY/ 26% MoM to NT$1.2tn, or 15% / 21% 
higher than our / Bloomberg consensus estimates, reflecting strong AI servers ramp 
up and new smartphone models in contribution. From a MoM perspective, 
Consumer electronics is the strongest, followed by Components, Cloud and 
Networking and Computing, and all segments are positive MoM. The strong MoM 
reaffirms our positive view on the company’s leading market position across: (1) 
Smartphone: serving global leading brands, which suffer less under high memory 
costs, with new model launches and form factor changes (report link), driving the 
shipment and ASP increase, (2) AI servers: serving global leading CSP customers, 
and early in the delivery of the latest AI server racks, driving the shipment and ASP 
increase. The company is also the major supplier of CPO switch, supporting Sep and 
4Q26 growth. From a YoY perspective, Cloud and Networking is the strongest, 
followed by Components, Computing, and Consumer electronics, and all segments 
are positive YoY. We attribute the slower YoY in consumer electronics to its major 
brand customers’ fewer model launches this year, along with the new form factor 
(foldable phones) being not available in Sep, and a modest smartphone market 
under high memory costs.

47%, or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both 
YoY and MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new 
smartphone models, and CPO switches. Management notes that AI-related products

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (unchanged) is derived from a peers’ 
average ratio of trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the 
sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our target P/E of 17.9x is at the high-end of the company’s 
historical forward P/E, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from 
the competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

EQUITY: HANDSETS
Largan Precision   3008.TW 3008 TT
Global Markets Research
5 October 2026
Highly sensitive to CPO FAU growth
CPO on track, likely to contribute c.18% of 2028F 
earnings; but near-term Apple demand could be lighter
Action: Maintain Buy; TP raised to TWD8,300, implying ~23% upside
We reiterate Buy on Largan, and raise our TP to TWD8,300, based on 30x 2028F EPS of 
TWD276.7 (previously: TWD6,000, on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD240.0). We cut our earnings 
forecasts by 7.2% and 10.4% for 2026F and 2027F, respectively, to reflect the adverse 
impact of rising BOM costs on smartphone demand and potential cannibalization of the 18 
Pro series by the iPhone Duo. For the first time we incorporate the co-packaged optics fiber 
array unit (CPO FAU) revenue into our earnings model, by assuming the new business 
would contribute 1%/18% of 2027/28F earnings. Hence, our 2028F earnings imply 44% 
y-y growth, driven by CPO FAU and Apple’s (AAPL US, Not rated) camera upgrades. We 
raise our target P/E from 25x to 30x to reflect Largan’s smooth progress in CPO FAU and a 
higher sensitivity to its earnings. Every one million units of CPO FAU could contribute 
c.TWD10 EPS to Largan, in our calculation. The target P/E of 30x exceeds the high end of 
the stock’s historical trading band (10-25x) since 2015. Largan trades at 35x 2027F P/E.
CPO FA/FAU progress on track
As we highlighted in June  and July , Largan can offer a full-range of CPO FAU components 
and modules, including prism/microlens array (PMLA), FA, and FAU, and has the strength to 
fully automate the FA and FAU assembly process by leveraging its strong internal machine 
tool capability. By doing so, Largan’s time-to-market and cost structures for FA and FAU 
assembly should be competitive vs peers. We estimate the total CPO FAU market could 
grow from less than 5mn units in 2027F to 30-40mn units in 2028F, and we expect 
Largan’s market share to be at 10%/15% in 2027/28F (Fig. 1 ). We assume the ASP to be 
over USD100, as its major customer will likely prefer Largan to handle FA/FAU assembly 
rather than just supply components. We believe its fully-automated pilot run line was 
ready by end-3Q26, and the first MP line could be ready by 1H27F. 
Apple: Sunny’s market share loss and continued camera upgrades in 2027-28F
Sunny (2382 HK, Neutral) earlier faced some yield issues with the new variable aperture (VA) 
main camera lens for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and this could benefit Largan and Genius 
(3406 TT, Buy). However, given the headwinds of rising memory costs and the potential 
cannibalization of the 18 Pro series by the iPhone Duo, we see slower iPhone lens shipments 
from end-2026F to 1H27F, before the 20th anniversary iPhone (Pro Max) and the second-gen 
iPhone Duo are introduced in 2H27F. We expect a further upgrade in iPhone Pro’s main 
camera in 2H27F, and introduction of a new periscope camera for the Pro Max in 2H28F.

Company profile
Largan is a technology leader in handset camera lenses, and is currently Top 2 plastic lens maker in the world.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD8,300 is based on 30x 2028F EPS of TWD276.7. Our target P/E of 30x exceeds the high end of the stock’s historical trading band
(10-25x) since 2015. The benchmark index for the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected sales of iPhones and Android phones; 2) level of inventory correction by Chinese customers; 3) higher-

than-expected ASP/margin erosion; 4) slower-than-expected spec upgrades; 5) increased competition, causing market share loss; and 6) worse-
than-expected production yields.
ESG
Largan focuses on resource recycling and voluntarily reported green house gas emission through attending ISO14064 in 2020. Largan has achieved
90%+ water recycling and targets to save 1% electricity every year.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, subject to 
limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an appropriate 
valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow analysis, expected 
return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated target price, defined as

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that the 
analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 'N/A' are 
not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging Markets (ex-Asia): 
MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and by 
other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
September  Sales  +22% MoM/
+206% YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• September revenue was NT$6,860mn (22% MoM/+206% YoY), a record
high.
• 3Q26 revenue came in at  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY),
significantly higher than  our estimate at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130%
YoY) and consensus at NT$14,555mn (+8% QoQ/+127% YoY).

Our view:
• We believe that the strength in 3Q26 was mainly driven by the
semiconductor and photonics business, especially SLT, metrology,
transceiver/CPO testing, etc.
• We think that Chroma might start to ship FT handlers and ovens in small
volume from late 2026/early 2027, which represent new business
opportunities for the coming years.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持奇鋐買進投資建議，並上修目標價，主係：(1) 4Q26 隨著
VR200 小量出貨，AWS T3 水冷及Google TPU 同步放量，營收與獲利
將逐季走高；(2)預期 AWS 及 AMD 將採用雙面水冷版，單價大幅提升；
(3)ASIC 產品毛利率高於GPU， 且預估27 年水冷營收比重將超過 5 成；
(4) NVIDIA CDU 認證名單及 Switch tray 規格的市場雜音，對基本
面影響有限。綜上所述，研究部上修 27 年營收至 3,409 億元(原估
3,144 億元)，EPS 上修至 180.69 元(原估 162.71 元)，給予 25 倍本
益比，目標價上修至 4,500 元，維持買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 4Q隨著VR小批量出貨，兩家ASIC放量，營運持續季增 
隨著GB300出貨持續強勁 ，VR小批量出貨， 研究部預期奇鋐9月
營收持續月增。展望4Q26，除了VR200板端開始小量出貨外，
Google TPU以及AWS T3水冷也將在4Q開始陸續放量，研究部微
幅調整4Q26營收至632億元(原估622億元)，季增11.9%，年增
32.3%。而VR200的出貨將持續到2Q27，3Q27將開始有小量Rubin 
ultra產品接續。

2. AWS及AMD採用雙面水冷板，散熱產值增加 
根據供應鏈調查， AWS T3水冷以及AMD MI系列將採用雙面式
水冷板，主因採用背面供電(主板空間不夠，記憶體模組移到
主板背面，故兩面都需水冷板散熱)，雙面式水冷板單價將有
望超過3000美元，產值大幅提升。此外，ASIC產品毛利率優於
GPU，公司產能也優先配置給ASIC，故研究部上修27年營收至
3409億元。

3. CDU認證及switch tray規格雜音，對基本面影響極小 
近期散熱有部分雜音：(1) NVIDIA DSX Ready 的CDU認證裡，
取得資格的業者為 LG Electronics、LiquidStack與Vertiv三
間，奇鋐未在首批名單中。研究部認為，目前奇鋐目前主力產
品仍為水冷板等零組件，CDU比較偏向系統級產品 ，27-28年才
會開始小量生產CDU ( 以In row 架構為主)，但目前營收比重
低，影響不大。(2)傳言27年Rubin Ultra 的Switch tray將從
18層改回9層設計，目前仍在討論中，但研究部 對奇鋐的27年
EPS預估，本身就未納入18層規格。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

4. 上修27年EPS預估，目標價上修至4500元  
除了GPU及ASIC需要水冷板之外，周圍相關產品也開始逐漸導
入液冷，如CPU/LPU Rack 、CPO等，奇鋐預估27年水冷營收比
重將超過5成。綜上所述 ，研究部微幅上修2026年營收至2,178
億元(原估2,168億元)， 年增56.0%；EPS 104.65元(原估104.02
元)，並上修2027年營收至3,409億元(原估3,144億元)，年增
56.5%，EPS上修至180.69元(原估162.71元)，給予25倍本益比 ，
目標價上修至4,500元，維持買進建議。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

穩懋 (3105 TT)  Buy

PIN 等光通訊訂單效應持續推升業績向上，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：穩懋 08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ，符合
群益先前預估， 3Q26 營收動能來自 optical 、 infrastructure ，預
估 09/2026 營收仍將較 08/2026 增加。就評價而言，穩懋受惠於
PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產，預估出貨量 4Q26 將
持續放大，且後續成長空間可期，為 4Q26 、2027 年重要營收動
能，故群益維持對穩懋 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 QoQ+83.22% ： 2Q26 營收 52.57 億元，
QoQ+14.54% ，動能來自 cellular 、infrastructure 、optical 等產品，
此外，雖然 optical 新產品量產初期良率較低，不過，由於 2Q26
產品結構優於 1Q26 、產能利用率提高，毛利率提高至 28.24% ，
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+72.14% ，加上認列 1.44 億元匯兌利益、
2.70 億元投資利益， 2Q26 稅後淨利增加至 9.77 億元，
QoQ+83.22% 。  
就 2Q26 的產品結構而言， cellular 產品營收 QoQ+18.00% ，原
因在於手機業者需求隨逐漸進入旺季而增溫； infrastructure 產
品營收 QoQ+14.53% ，動能來自 AI data center 、低軌道衛星；  
optical 產品 2Q26 重要動能有二， (1) 新款 iPhone 相關 VCSEL
訂單數量增加， (2)PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產。  
PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大為後續重要營收動能： 展望
3Q26 ， optical 產品受惠於 PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大，營
收成長性居各產品之冠，預估將帶動 others 產品營收
QoQ+35.97% ，且後續成長空間仍可期；其次， infrastructure 產
品出貨動能來自低軌道衛星標案陸續出貨，預估相關營收
QoQ+20.45% ，由於全球各地低軌道衛星標案隨應用領域拓展而
有機會持續釋出，後續營收前景亦仍佳。  
至於 cellular 、 Wi-Fi 產品出貨成長性雖受到近期記憶體缺料等
因素影響整體手機產業景氣而存在不確定性，但現階段預估營
收均與 2Q26 實績相去有限。

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，穩懋持續透過各項節能措
施提升廠內能源使用效率，以減少用電度數，且新設產線全面採用最佳可行
技術，實際措施包括 PFCs 尾氣處理設備全面採用去除效率達 90% 以上之型
式 (IPCC 公告指定型式 )、逐步汰換既設產線之 PFCs 尾氣處理設備、評估製
程氣體減量使用或取代高潛勢溫室氣體使用、採取工程改善或設備更換以
降低用電量、評估新建廠房設置廠內再生能源等。  
再就企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果而言，穩懋致力於新技
術的開發，持續推出高效能的技術平台，以生產更具能源效益和節能的半導
體通訊產業產品，因終端產品應用逐漸擴及手機、無線網路、雷達、衛星等
各個通訊領域，有助於全球能源節省和減少用電消耗。此外，藉由穩懋多功
能的晶片整合、製程線寬的縮減以及製程能力的提升，終端產品之尺寸、單
位晶片使用量均持續減少，亦得以為守護地球環境盡一份心力。

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 5 日

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Cerdia 100% 股 權 。 交 易 架 構 由 兩 部 分 構 成， 材料 -KY 以 自 有 資 金 支 付 現
金 對 價 約 美 元 2.14 億 ， 佔 比 約  58% ； 其 餘 發 行 新 股 109,295 仟股 ， 佔比
約  42% ， 交 易 完 成 後 賣 方 黑 石 集 團 實 體 將 持 有 材 料 -KY 增 資 後 約  9.95% 
股 權 ， 集 團 股 本 由 9.89 億 股 增 至 10.98 億股。   
被 收 購 方  Cerdia 2025 年 營 收 為 美 元  7.31 億 ， 調 整 後  EBITDA 為 美 元  
2.67 億， EBITDA 利潤率為  36.5% 。 截 至  06/2026 之近  12 個 月 ， 雙 方
合 計 營 收 約 達 10 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 達 4 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 利
潤率為  39% 。 此 交 易 使 材 料-KY 由 原 中 國 山 東 單 一 生 產 基 地 ， 擴 增 至 德 國
弗萊堡、美國金斯波特、巴西聖安德烈及俄羅斯謝爾普霍夫等全球五大基
地 。 合 併 後 全 球 醋 酸 纖 維 絲 束 設 計 產 能 達 16.40 萬噸 /年 ， 醋 酸 纖 維 素 醋 片
設 計 產 能 達 19.50 萬噸 /年。   
財 務 結 構 改 善 為 本 次 收 購 的 核 心 獲 利 驅 動 力 。Cerdia 目 前 帳 上 存 續 美 元 9 
億 私 債，到 期 日 為 2031 年，票 面 利 率 約 9%，年 利 息 支 出 達 美 元 0.81 億。
管 理 層 規 劃 於  2027 年 交 割 並 表 後 進 行 債 務 再 融 資 ， 透 過 台 系 與 歐 系 銀 行
將 利 率 降 至 4.5% –6% ， 預 估 每 年 節 省 約 美 元 0.41 億 利 息 支 出 ， 扣 稅 後 將
直 接 挹 注 合 併 淨 利 。 
材料 *-KY 2Q26 毛 利 率 降 至 40.9% ，主 因 山 東 各 廠 進 行 3 週至  1 個 月 歲
修 ， 停 工 固 定 費 用 全 數 列 入 當 期 銷 貨 成 本 ，3Q26 毛 利 率 已 回 升 至 近 五 成 ；
3Q26 另 認 列 自 山 東 匯 回 美 元 3 億 資 本 之 10% 一 次 性 所 得 稅， 約 為 0.30 
億美元 (約 新 台 幣 9.6 億元 )， 不 影 響 本 業 長 期 獲 利 。  
產業與產品布局上，全球絲束市場由五大業者濃縮為四大業者。 公司 採取
調控供給策略，將山東部分產能轉向長絲、短絲及不織布，維持絲束市場
供給緊縮以支撐產品價格。收購宣布後引發下游避險訂單，公司採取控制
接單策略，將談判遞延至  10/2026 至  11/2026 進行  2027 年 合 約 調 價 。
產品分工方面，材料 -KY 負 責 長 絲 、 短 絲 及 塑 膠 級 醋 片 ， 切 入 紡 織 、 眼 鏡
框與  AI 穿戴材料； Cerdia 則主打  85 種濾嘴規格及  100% 生物可降解
濾 嘴。材 料 *-KY 具 備 眼 鏡 膠 框 熱 解 回 收 專 利，正 與 東 莞 大 型 眼 鏡 廠 合 作 處
理每年約  1.65 萬 噸 廢 料 ， 預 計 12/2026 正 式 公 告 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
展望 2H26~2027 年， 2Q26 本 業 表 現 強 勁 ， 上 半 年 EPS 創 歷史 新高 。 8 月
營收 持 續 成 長，成 長 動 能 以 低 軌 衛 星 及 航 太 國防 為主 ，公司 已 通過 ITA R（國

其中 航 太 國 防 ＋ 低 軌 衛 星 合 計 佔 過 半，且 持 續 向 高 毛 利 應 用 集 中。 預估 2027
年 稅 後 EPS 6.17 元， PE 約 16.6 X， 考 量 反 無 人 機 系 統 急 單 持 續 ， 客 戶 要 求
產 能 加 倍 ， LEO 隨 客 戶 新 發 射 計 畫 推 進 ， 建 議 強 力 買 進 ， 目 標價為 155 元
(25 * 2027 年 EPS) 。

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介

專 注 於 高 混 、 中 低 量 產 的 嵌 入 式 系 統 與 精 密 組 裝 。 主要業務 包括 a. 航太／
國 防 ／ 低 軌 道 衛 星 （ 目 前 營 收 主 力，約 佔 較 高 比 重 ） ：線 束 （ca ble h arn ess ）、
機 箱 佈 線 組 件、鈑 金、電 纜 訂 製、 UL 安 全 帶、精 密 組 裝 等，具 備 A S9100D 、

娛 樂 ： 博 弈 板 卡 、 準 系 統 、 玩 家 追 蹤 系 統 、 機 台 框 體 等 。 外 銷 佔 比 極 高（ 約
99% 以 上 ， 美 洲 為 主，約 98% ） ， 內 銷 極 低 。 近 年 航 太 ／ 國 防 ／ 低 軌 衛 星
比 重 提 升 ， 博 弈 與 工 業 電 腦 仍 佔 重 要 地 位 。 ITA R 認 證 與 美 國 在 地 生 產 形 成

鏡 頭 、 手 臂 等 ） ， 非 地 面 接 收 器 。 需 符 合 航 太 等 級 規 格 ， 是 衛 星 關 鍵 部
件。

單 ， 預 計 4Q26 正 式 出 貨 。 若 驗 證 順 利 ， 後 續 量 產 訂 單 可 望 大 幅 爆 發 。

需 求 已 排 至 2027 年， 27F 營收 YOY+30% 。  
主 要 產 品 包 括 ： a. 軍 用 穿 戴 裝 置 : 用 在 作 戰 人 員 胸 前 ／ 手 腕 式 裝 置 （ 亦 可 延
伸 至 消 防 、 警 察 、 救 援 ）； b. 反 無 人 機 系 統 ： 因 美 伊 相 關 衝 突 導 致 客 戶 庫 存
大 量 消 耗 ， 急 單 湧 現 ， 要 求 加 大 產 能 、 最快速度出貨 ； c.  無 人 機 控 制 相 關
電 腦 零 件 ： 2025 年 切 入 波 音 無 人 機 供 應 鏈 ， 2026 年 開 始 正 式 放 量 ； 目 前
小 量 後 階 段 ， 實 際 大 量 拉 貨 自 Q3 起 ， 需 求 已 排 至 2027 年 ； d. 海 事 監 控 無
人 機 與 建 控 系 統 、 攝 像 系 統（ 標 準 偵 測 距 離 20 公 里 、 長 程 30 公 里 以 上 ） 。 
核心 優 勢 ： 與 美 國 國 防 大 客 戶 合 作 超 過 16 年 ， 近 期 獲 頒 優 良 供 應 商 獎 。 公
司 可 提 供 從 PCBA 到 完 整 機 電 整 合 、 cable harn ess 的 整 套 解 決 方 案 ， 整 合
多 供 應 商 能 力 ， 協 助 客 戶 降 本 並 保 留 毛 利 。

受 惠 低 軌 衛 星 及 航 太 / 國防 帶 動 ， 預 估 2027 年 營 收 持 續 成 長  
2Q26 營收 13.77 億 元 ， 季 增 約 30.2% 、 年 增 約 76.3 %。 主 因 國 防 軍 工 與 低

5.4 4 元 超 越 歷 年 全 年 度 水 準 ， 刷 新 上 市 以 來 歷 史 新 高 。

約 40% 、 通 訊 （ 含 低 軌 衛 星 ） 約 18% 、 博 弈 約 17% 。 低 軌 衛 星 為 重 要 成 長

束 需 求 ） 。公 司 已 斬 獲AS T S pa ce  Mobile 關 鍵 零 組 件 試 產 訂 單，預 計 4Q26
出 貨 。

關 稅 風 險 。 另 外 ，美 墨 雙 據 點 可 彈 性 配 置 ， 高 自 動 化 ／ 高 機 密 產 品 留 美 國 ，
勞 動 密 集（ 如 線 束 ）移 墨 西 哥 ， 優 化 成 本 與 交 期 。 目 前 利 用 率 約 7 成 左 右 ，
在 新 訂 單 帶 動 下 ， 預估 後續 營收 有 機 會 倍增 。

公 司 管 理 層 看 好 下 半 年 營 運 優 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 本 業 強 勁 成 長 ， 毛 利 率 可 望
維持 20 ~21 % 左右，獲利顯著優於過往年度。另積極布局無人載具等新領
域。 長 線 預 期 衛 星 成 長 趨 勢 明 確 ， 2027>2026>2025 年 ， 相 關 訂 單 動 能 持

漲價紅利延續；特殊材料蓄勢待發 
 漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升：2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要
受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現跳升。M6 以下、無鉛、
無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，騰輝 2026 年一般材料
價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9 月份一般材
料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有
產品線毛利率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻。 
 深化特殊材料布局 ， 期待新專案驅動中長線成長：截至 3Q26 VT-901
產品營收+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體
設備大廠將 VT-901 用於半導體老化測試板。近期騰輝新增 8～9 個
散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊材料成
長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL
與 PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。 
 買進評等：預期一般材料漲價效益可延續至 2027 年，且若新專案驗
證順利，有望進一步推升產品組合。預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
15.12/30.86 元，評等買進，目標價 460 元(15x2027EPS)。

個股報告 
騰輝電子-KY 產品主要分一般材
料與特殊材料

騰輝電子-KY 主要從事印刷電路板所需之黏合片、銅箔 基板及鋁基板生產與銷
售，一般材料產品應用於光電板、汽車板等中低階產品，特殊材料主要為散熱
鋁基板，另外亦有著墨軍工航天等利基型領域， 2025 年產品組合：一般材料
41%、特殊材料 53%、其他 6%；2026 年一般材料受惠漲價、缺貨，預估2026
年產品組合：一般材料 53%、特殊材料 41%、其他 6%。騰輝總部位於中國蘇
州，主要生產基地位亦位於中國蘇州(月產能 80 萬張)、江陰(月產能 20 萬張)、
深圳(月產能 10 萬張)與台灣(月產能 10 萬張)， 泰國廠(月產能 10 萬張)則於 2026
年 9 月開始量產。

漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升  騰輝 2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現
跳升。CCL 上游原料方面，E 布維持逐月漲價，而銅箔供給緊張程度僅次於玻
纖布；CCL 方面，CCL 龍頭廠商將產能集中於 M6 以上高速材料，中低階 CCL
供給明顯收縮，M6 以下、無鉛、無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，
騰輝 2026 年一般材料價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9
月份一般材料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有產品線毛利
率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻；綜上所述，預估3Q26/4Q26 毛利
率將分別提升至 39.8%/41.6%， 推估3Q26/4Q26 EPS 分別為 4.38 元/6.04 元，
合計推估 2026 全年 EPS 為 15.12 元。

深化特殊材料布局，期待新專案
驅動中長線成長 
 騰輝長期聚焦利基型特殊材料市場，並具備自行合成樹脂能力，除了掌握關鍵
配 方 外 ， 亦 可 降 低 原 料 成 本 。PI 方 面 ， 截 至 3Q26 V T-901 產 品 營 收
+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體設備大廠將 VT-901
用於半導體老化測試板，PI 產品毛利率約有 70%高水準；其他高毛利率產品如
不流膠 PP 營收亦有好表現，截至 3Q26 不流膠 PP 營收+50%YOY。另外，騰
輝為全球主要車燈散熱材料供應商，其散熱鋁基板 80%應用於汽車照明，近期
騰輝新增 8～9 個散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊
材料成長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL 與
PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。產能方面，目前稼動率水準
未達滿載以提供訂單彈性；泰國新廠今年 9 月開始試加工，初期由裁切等後段
製程開始，上膠機預計 1H27 進廠裝機，完整產線預計 2027 年到位，目前規劃
至少兩條產線，若全產能運行，預估可增加 10～15%產能，除支應既有客戶需
求外，亦為 2027 年新產品放量預留成長空間；此外，騰輝亦通過 15 億元昆山
擴建案，新產能亦預計於 2027 年展開貢獻。

3Q26/4Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 主要成長動能來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及
AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，高階產品規格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升，其中
1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以上。BT 載板則持續
受惠記憶體需求強勁。樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產能均已接近全面
滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率 60%–70%以上，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。本中心
預估 3Q/4Q26 營收 163.8/188.5 億元，季增 20.6%/15.1% ，毛利率
30%/32.3%，稅後獲利 35/46.3 億元，EPS 5.43/7.16 元。 
玻纖布、CCL 持續漲價，預期 4Q26–2027 年載板價格上漲 
經本中心供應鏈訪查了解，高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，使部分 BT 料
件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上；ABF 隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上
Build-up 結構發展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；
玻纖布方面， Nittobo 新增玻纖布供給已大致完成客戶分配，短期缺口難以
明顯緩解。隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，
持續消耗載板廠商有效產能。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價由規格升級、單顆材
料用量增加與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至
2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
2027 年營運動能明顯增強，給予買進評等，目標價 1,700 元 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過 468 億元資本支出計畫，主要鎖定大尺寸、高層數、
高精密度 IC 載板。在高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動
下，預期 ABF/BT 毛利率可望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。
本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1,095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，
毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。本中心給予南電買進評等，以
2027 年 EPS，30 倍本益比推得目標價 1,700 元。

元大觀點 
⧫  
台灣載板產能接近全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率提升至 60%–70%且
4Q26 有望滿載，預估 3Q/4Q26 營收季增 20.6%/15.1%。 
⧫  
規格升級與高階 產品及上游原料 產能不足共同驅動載板漲價，預期
ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
⧫  
高階產品比重提升、稼 動率維持高檔及報價上調，給予 南電 買進評
等，目標價 1,700 元，以 2027 年 EPS 56.78 元及 30 倍本益比推估。

營運分析 
3Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 營收 163.8 億元，季增 20.6%，年增 49.3%，主要成長動能仍來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 載
板方面，受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及 AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，除出貨量持續增加外，高階產品規
格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升。其中，1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以
上，隨高階產品占比提升，為營收與獲利成長的推力。BT 載板則持續受惠 DRAM 與 NAND Flash
等記憶體需求強勁，加上上游原材料成本上漲逐步反映於產品報價，使 BT 載板呈現量價齊揚。毛
利率預估為 30%，營業利益 43.7 億元，季增 50.6%，年增 604.1%，稅後獲利 35 億元，季增 55%，
年增 383.6%，EPS 5.43 元。

台灣廠全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率快速回升 
產能利用率方面，目前台灣主要載板廠區維持高檔運作，其中樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產
能均已接近全面滿載，顯示高階載板及記憶體相關需求仍相當強勁。同時，昆山 ABF 廠則為後續
營運改善的重要來源。隨美系網通大廠新專案自 7M26 正式進入量產，昆山 ABF 稼動率已由 2Q26
約 50%快速提升至 60%–70%，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。伴隨稼動率提高及良率改善，昆山廠毛
利率亦顯著提升，預期後續貢獻幅度將提升。PCB 業務，稼動率正由 70%–75%向上，主因 AI 伺
服器板與記憶體模組板需求驅動。綜合上述，預期 4Q26 營收 188.5 億元，季增 15.1%，年增
68.8%，毛利率 32.3%，營業利益 55.1 億元，季增 26.1%，年增 472.1%，稅後獲利 46.3 億元，
季增 32%，EPS 7.16 元

玻纖布、 CCL 等價格仍持續漲價，預期 4Q26-2027 年載板價格上漲 
隨 AI GPU 、ASIC、高速交換器及 HBM 等應用快速成長，IC 載板規格持續朝大尺寸、高層數、細
線路、低翹曲及高耐熱發展，帶動材料由一般電子級產品轉向特種玻纖布、高階 BT 樹脂、ABF 增
層膜等高規材料。經本中心近期供應鏈訪查及日本材料展資訊整理，三菱瓦斯化學（ MGC）指出，
過去 BT 載板主要應用於 DRAM、NAND Flash 及消費性 IC，但隨 HBM、AI ASIC 、網通晶片與
CPO 導入，材料須進一步滿足低 CTE、高耐熱及高速電性需求，高階 BT 與傳統產品的規格差距
持續擴大，目前高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，部分料件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上。玻纖
布方面，Nittobo 目前產能維持 100%滿載，雖規劃 2027–2028 年新增產能，但新增供給已大致完
成客戶分配，短期缺口難以明顯緩解。ABF 方面，隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上 Build-up 結構發
展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；同時 Resonac 相關載板材料報價已調
升約 20%–30%。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價並非單純景氣循環，而是規格升級、單顆材料用量增加
與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF／BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第4 頁，共 19頁  
圖 3：載板面積及層數增加亦在 Ajinomoto 未來載板發展藍圖

資料來源：Ajinomoto，元大投顧整理

南電此前已宣布 468 億元資本支出，為創立以來單筆最大投資 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過新一輪資本支出計畫，總投資金額達 468 億元，為公司成立以來最大單筆
投資案，規模亦超過 2018–2022 年上一輪擴產期間累計資本支出。其中約 110 億元投入廠房土建，
358 億元用於設備採購，新廠定位為具備 Class 100 無塵室等級的高階智慧工廠，主要鎖定大尺寸、
高層數、高精密度 IC 載板，以及 CoWoS、CoPoS 等先進封裝衍生需求。顯示此次投資並非既有
產線單純擴充，而是針對下一世代高階載板重新建置生產平台。整體而言 ，廠區調配方面，錦興廠
P2 升級已於 1H26 完成，主要提升自動化程度與無塵室規格，以因應大型、高階載板生產；樹林
廠則因應 2027 年美系客戶 Server CPU 需求成長，陸續將部分既有成熟產品轉往昆山生產，以釋
放台灣高階產能供新產品使用。

展望 2026-2027 年，ABF/BT 料源及載板的產能供應缺口將推升載板廠商營運 
AI 需求正在從「訓練」往「推論」延伸，這會讓晶片種類變多、數量變大，進而同時推升 IC 載板
的量與規格。此前從 Deep Learning 、Perceptual AI 一路到 Generative AI、Agentic AI，未來再往
Physical AI 發展。訓練（Learning）主要依賴大型模型運算，因此核心晶片以高效能 GPU 為主；
但到了推論（Inference）階段，更重視即時反應速度、功耗與成本，因此除了 GPU 之外，CPU 與
ASIC 的使用比例會明顯提高。展望 2026/2027 年，預計南電載板稼動率持續改善，其中 ABF 與
BT 均已進入高檔運作。ABF 方面，台灣樹林及錦興廠稼動率已接近滿載；昆山廠則為 2H26 稼動
率回升的主要來源，受惠美系網通客戶及中國客戶量產，ABF 稼動率由 2Q26 約 50%快速提升至
3Q26 的 60%–70%，預期 4Q26 在供給持續吃緊下，可進一步提升，2027 年初亦將因應美系客戶
Server CPU 需求快速成長。BT 載板供需則更加緊張，除現有 DRAM、SSD 相關需求持續強勁，
另一方面，部分同業逐步將 BT 產能轉向 ABF，韓系供應商亦優先支應集團內需求，市場有效供給
持續收縮，景碩亦表示稼動率也將逐步向上，預期 2027 年 BT 仍將維持高稼動率。 
綜合上述，本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率
26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年
增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第5 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
AI 需求增溫造成 T-glass 玻纖布供應吃緊，助益近期 BT 載板報價調整 
低熱膨脹係數（Low CTE ）玻璃纖維布，簡稱 T-glass，為高階 AI ABF 載板及高階 BT 載板重要原
料，由 Nittobo(日東紡)獨家供應，並由日系廠商 Resonac(ABF 載板 CCL 主要供應商)、三菱瓦斯
(BT 載板 CCL 主要供應商)採用。由於高階 AI 晶片需求增溫帶動高階 AI ABF 載板出貨成長，造成
T-glass 供應吃緊並連帶使高階 BT 載板 CCL(終端用途包含記憶體控制器、手機 Ap、Sip 等)交貨
期拉長，T-glass 玻纖布供應正常時高階 BT 載板 CCL 交貨期約 8-10 週，後因供應吃緊造成交貨期
拉長至 16-20 週，目前因玻纖布供應更為緊張已無告知交貨期。 
經供應鏈調查，台系載板廠已於 2025 年 7 月開始針對部分採用 T-glass 之 BT 載板產品調漲價格，
此外 BT 載板亦有載板廠進行價格調漲，以反應高階玻纖布供應日漸緊缺，以及年初以來銅價、金
價等原物料成本上漲壓力。由於 BT 載板產業長期以來供過於求，且當前高階 AI 晶片普遍採用
ABF 載板，多數載板廠此前 BT 業務毛利率較為偏低，淡季時季毛利率多有承壓。本中心認為漲價
趨勢可延續，將有助載板廠獲利改善。

圖 6：金價近一年走勢

資料來源：Cmoney、元大投顧整理

中長期高階 ABF 載板價格或能進一步調升 
受惠 AI 發展浪潮帶動 AI GPU 、AI ASIC 等 AI 晶片需求成長，AI ABF 載板需求正向。本中心推測
短期高階 AI ABF 載板因獲利水準足夠暫無調漲壓力，但中長期隨需求持續上升、載板面積增大、
T-glass 供應持續吃緊，AI ABF 載板產品台系廠商後續仍有進一步議價空間。目前欣興、南電、景
碩皆有耕耘高階 ABF 載板，例如欣興在美系 GPU Blackwell 市佔率預估為 30-40%，且為全球主要
AI ASIC 載板供應商；南電今年 800G 載板比重預期約 7%，包含美系、非美系等終端需求；景碩
則有望成為美系 AI GPU 載板第三供。此外部分載板廠因原物料上漲等成本壓力在 2Q25、3Q25 小
幅調整中低階 ABF 載板報價，本中心判斷若 T-glass 供應持續吃緊並排擠 E-glass 產能，則後續中
低階 ABF 載板價格有望進一步提升。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第7 頁，共 19頁  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 91%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic) 、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國四大 CSP 資本支出將年增 91%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，NVIDIA
加 AMD GPU 預估年增 17%及 34%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用
GPU 算力需求仍維持成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者， 2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下， CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本， ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

圖 9：GPU/ASIC 出貨量預估

資料來源：元大投顧

AI 晶片面積持續擴大，將使 ABF 載板用量大增 
隨著大型語言模型與生成式 AI 的算力需求快速攀升，AI 晶片的設計持續朝向更高運算密度與更大
面積發展。無論是 NVIDIA、AMD 或各家大廠的新世代加速器，都呈現晶粒尺寸擴大、功耗上升、
I/O 腳位數倍增的趨勢。在此背景下，ABF 載板的需求不僅以「量」擴張，更以「規格」升級為主
軸。為支撐大型 GPU/AI ASIC 的高頻寬連接與多晶片架構，ABF 層數、線寬線距、孔徑控制及翹
曲管理均需同步提升，帶動高端載板技術門檻不斷拉高。由於先進 AI 晶片的封裝面積更大、電源
與訊號整合更複雜，單顆晶片所需 ABF 載板面積與材料用量明顯增加。整體而言，AI 晶片規模擴
大正驅動 ABF 產業進入新一輪升級周期，高階需求將成為未來成長的主要動能。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第10 頁，共 19頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
給予南電買進評等，目標價 1700 元 
本中心維持 2026–2027 年載板廠商將持續受惠於高稼動率與漲價趨勢的看法不變。主因新世代高
階載板規格持續大型化，傳統 PC／NB 載板尺寸多約 27×27mm，而 Server CPU 、AI ASIC 及高
速交 換器 載板 已逐 步 放大 至 70–80mm 、100mm 以 上， 部分 大型 產品 甚 至 朝 140×140mm 、
150×150mm 發展，未來更大型先進封裝架構更可能逼近 200mm 等級。由於載板生產係以固定尺
寸 Panel 進行製造，隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，同時邊角無
法有效利用所造成的 Area Loss 亦明顯增加，此將持續消耗載板廠商有效產能，有助產業維持高稼
動率，並支撐 2026–2027 年載板報價向上。本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，
年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，
稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。 
綜合上述，南電高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動下，預期 ABF／BT 毛利率可
望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。評價方面，南電歷史本益比交易區間約介於 6–36
倍；考量本輪 ABF／BT 供需結構改善、高階產品組合升級，以及稼動率快速提升所帶來的毛利率
擴張，本中心給予 2027 年 30 倍本益比評價，以 2027 年預估 EPS 推算目標價 1,700 元，並給予
買進投資建議。

公司簡介  
南亞電路板成立於 1997 年，前身為南亞塑膠自 1985 年開始營運的電路板事業部，1998 年正式獨立營運，並於 2006 年在
台灣證券交易所掛牌上市，為台塑集團旗下主要 PCB 與 IC 載板廠商，目前南亞塑膠持股約 67%。南亞電路板     年報
_8046 南電_2025 年報(1) 公司產品涵蓋傳統多層印刷電路板、HDI、軟硬複合板（Rigid-Flex），以及 ABF 與 PP／BT IC
載板，生產據點主要位於台灣錦興、樹林及中國昆山。南亞電路板 其中 IC 載板為公司主要高階產品布局，應用涵蓋伺服器
CPU、AI ASIC、高效能運算（HPC）、800G／1.6T 高速交換器、記憶體、SSD 控制晶片、SiP、行動裝置及車用電子等
領域；一般 PCB 則持續往 AI 伺服器、GPU 加速卡、伺服器與高階網通等高附加價值產品升級。    年報_8046 南電_2025
年報(1) 公司近年營運重心進一步向 AI 與 HPC 相關高階載板移動，受惠 AI 晶片朝大尺寸、高層數及高 I/O 規格發展，以及
CSP 持續擴大自研 ASIC 布局，南電持續強化高階 ABF 載板、1.6T 交換器及 AI 伺服器相關產品布局，並規劃新增高階載
板產能，以支應下一階段 AI 基礎建設需求。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南電整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先
的位置，在電子零組件行業的公司中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南電的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略為領先電子零組件行業的平均水準。  
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南電在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司訊息揭露能力非常優良，
提供投資人及市場充足的資訊。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

Goldilocks 
We resume coverage of the PCB/CCL industry, focusing primarily on Google’s TPU supply 
chain, which we believe will deliver the strongest and most sustainable growth through 2028 
and beyond. We forecast aggregate TPU volumes to more than triple between 2025/26 and 
2028, far outpacing competing platforms. Critically, we project a surge in the content value 
of next-generation TPU ASICs. As Google integrates more advanced logic and HBM chiplets 
into a single compute module, we project TPU content value to increase five- to tenfold over 
the next three to four years.  Accordingly, we estimate that the addressable PCB content 
value alone could quadruple from 2026 to 2029. Equally vital is Google’s longstanding 
leadership in optical connectivity for its Superpods. The upcoming doubling of switch speeds 
and interconnect bandwidth will act as a secondary tailwind, further lifting PCB content 
value. Consequently, we expect the PCB/CCL market to experience continuous pricing power 
and a possibility of LTAs forming, mirro ring the supply dynamics seen in the memory 
industry. Within the key TPU vendor matrix, we resume coverage of Isu Petasys and EMC with 
BUY ratings and target prices of KRW 270k and TWD 10,000, respectively. We also initiate 
coverage of TTMI with a BUY rating and a target price of $236. Risks include end demand, 
execution, and US policy on AI data centre expansion. 
Why Google’s TPUs?  
We forecast TPU’s volume to more than triple to 12m units in 2028 (8.5mn in 2027) from 3.6mn 
in 2026, encompassing both in-house deployment and allocations to third-party AI labs via ASIC 
services. The strong volume is evident by its massive $520bn cloud service backlog orders in 
C2Q26. At the same time, TPU is expected to quadruple in TPU V9 (mid-28) vs V8 (2H26) and 
could double again in V10 (2029) as Google squeezes more chiplets into its ASIC designs. The 
complexity of design for PCB (upgrade to 50L+ or HDI) and CCL (upgrade to M8/M9 material or 
even PTFE) represents at least 50%-100% expansion in ASP in each generation. Furthermore, 
networking is expected to see a similar upgrade trend in PCB/CCL, as scale-up architectures 
and bandwidth requirement rise, especially from 400Gbps to 800G/1.6Tbps.    
LTA likely becomes new norm under strong demand and supply constraint  
Our supply chain survey indicated increasing discussion about long-term agreements (LTA) 
over the PCB and CCL supply chain with CSPs. Terms may be different from vendor to vendor, 
and most of them are still under discussion at this stage. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and LTAs 
for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain undersupplied. 
Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash flow, 2) make the 
supply chain more willing to invest given longer visibility and secured shipment, and 3) improve 
supply chain management with better profitability. We believe this will further boost P/E for the 
supply chain, if LTAs are confirmed, with longer visibility and stable order backlogs.   
We prefer TPU supply chain into 2027/28: buy on EMC, ISU and TTM 
The TPU supply chain will be the biggest winner into 2027/28 with strong unit growth and 
content value upgrades. Shipments are expected to reach 8.5/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. 
Content value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs 
USD20k/7k in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in v9 with HDI adoption. We like EMC for its 
leading position and expect TPU/800Gbps switches to become the company’s key earnings 
driver. We set our TP at NT$10,000, with 30x FY27/28 PER. ISU is uniquely positioned to capture 
these secular trends due to its high exposure to Google and networking. We have a BUY and a 
TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 PER. Lastly, TTM is the best proxy in the United States to 
capture both AI server and national defence trends with better profitability and irreplaceability 
given its industry position. We initiate with BUY with a TP of USD236 with 30x FY27/28 PER. Title

4 October 2026  3 
GPU/ASIC volume, content value and price hikes drive 
PCB/CCL to another strong cycle in 2027/28 
We are positive on the PCB/CCL sector into 2027/28, with a strong upgrade cycle, and the 
whole industry will remain undersupplied because our checks indicate 1) continued AI device 
unit growth, supported by increasing CSP capex, 2) PCB design complexity will boost content 
value (increasing processing time and lowering yield rate), 3) CCL material upgrade to 
support higher data transmission and power supply, and 4) price hikes in the near-term 
because of the supply shortage and LTAs in the longer-term for a better demand outlook. 
What is not in the price yet?  
The sector has outperformed year-to-date, but we believe the PCB/CCL upgrade cycle will 
continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI device unit growth (especially Google 
TPU, of which we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant content value growth via 
PCB design complexity (higher layer counts, advanced form factor like HDI/mSAP, longer 
processing time) and CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switch, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB s to support  
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs.  
The overall industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end 
products, with the key bottleneck being in upstream material (CCL, glass fiber and copper foil) 
as well as PCB equipment, based on our supply chain checks. The longest lead-time for 
certain PCB equipment (like drilling machines) can be up to 36 months, suggesting strong 
surge in demand from the supply chain. We don’t see any sign of the tightness easing in the 
near term.  
AI server unit growth and spec upgrade in 2027/28   
We estimate GPU & ASIC volume will grow approximately 50% in CY2027 and CY2028, 
reaching a potential 34mn units in CY28, up from 15m units in CY26. The key driver is the 
expected unit surge in both CSPs’ ASICs as well as AI labs, that is, OpenAI and Anthropic. Their 
aggregate volume is forecast to rise from 5.5mn –6mn in CY26 to reach 12mn -13mn units in 
CY26 and further increase to 20mn in CY28, implying a quadrupling in a three-year period. 
Some of the notable drivers including the following: 
▶OpenAI’s direct procurement compute power (excluding CPS and neoclouds) is expected 
to reach 11Gw in CY28, up from 3.3GW in CY27 and a mere 1GB in CY26. This includes AVGO’s-
sponsored ASIC of 1.3GW (Jalapeno) and 5GW in CY27/28 respectively, while the remaining 
goes to AMD (8GW in the three-year period) and, to a lesser extent, NVDA; 
▶Similarly, Anthropic’s own compute capacity is likely to reach 13GW in CY28, up sharply 
from CY27’s 6GW. The majority of this comes from AVGO (TPU V8i and V8.5) while 5GW is 
supplied by AMD of  in two years; 
▶Meta is likely to build 3GW of its own compute on its ASIC (via AVGO) in CY27/28 using its 
MTIA400/450 ASIC. We note Meta’s previous ASIC program in 2025/26 has been scaled back 
and pushed out so this marks the second attempt at ramping up its own silicon solution.   
▶We estimate Google’s own compute TPU volume is expected to increase to 5.5mn units in 
CY28 from 4mn -4.5mn in CY27 and ~3.5mn in both CY25/26. While the unit growth appears to 
be modest relative to the AI labs, we note the compute capacity is likely to surge given the 
TDP for V8 and V9 is materially higher than those of V6/7.  
While NVDA’s GPU volume is likely to moderate and market share is expected to decline (as 
most of its customers are building their own ASIC and diversifying to other suppliers), it will 
remain the dominant supplier to most CSPs, neoclouds and enterprises. Moreover, NVDA, 
along with industry is migrating to a multi-rack or large pod structure to offer aggregated

and networking racks sustain its competitive edge in the fast-moving environment.

AI server performance to jump significantly every generation 
As shown in the table below, we see huge upgrades in compute power and TDP every 
generation. PCBs and CCL will upgrade along with AI server specs to reduce data loss and 
improve signal performance. PCB design is getting more complex from 20L -30L to 50L+ in the 
future or the structure will likely be migrated from HLT to HDI. For CCL, we witness a clear 
trend of upgrade material from M7 to M8 now and will see more adoption of the M8.5/M9 
going forward, boosting the ASP (a doubling from M7 to M8; M8 to M9 also doubling).  
In the future, along with strong agentic AI demand, we believe AI server design will further 
advance exponentially in the next two to three years. TPU’s TDP is expected to grow 10x from 
v8 to v10 within two to three years to further drive-up PCB/CCL’s content value significantly. 
AMD will equip PCBs with 50L+ into the MI500 generation and Open AI’s aggressive hardware 
production pipeline will provide upside surprise to the market. We can expect PCB/CCL to 
upgrade accordingly. We will have a more comprehensive analysis in the next section.

Summary: PCB/CCL spec upgrade and key suppliers in AI solutions  
The table below shows the key players’ solution and their PCB/CCL specifications as well as 
major suppliers. For PCB, we see an increasing layer count from 20L–30L now to 50L+ in the 
coming years and moving from HLC to HDI for more complex design. Our supply chain check 
suggests AMD’s MI500 series as well as Nvidia’s LPU PCB will likely adopt 50L+ design. This 
represents a longer processing time with a lower yield rate, and design complexity will 
support further ASP hikes. For CCL, M8 is becoming a standard material for AI 
servers/networking and we are going to see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). 
CCL is the most critical component in PCB and every generation upgrade (M7 to M8, or M8 to 
M9) implies the ASP will nearly double.    
Nvidia: It started to adopt M8 material from the GB200 NVL72. Rubin is expected to bring 
another modest upgrade: 1) introducing mid-plane with 44L design and M8 (or M8.5) CCL 
material, 2) most of the rest PCB/CCL remain on M8 with higher layer counts. The company is 
evaluating PTFE material for its switch board in the next generation. Also, we think Nvidia’s 
LPU can generate incremental demand for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. 
PCB suppliers for LPU will be VGT, WUS and TTM mainly, while, in our view, CCL suppliers are 
Shengyi (major) and EMC.  
Google: TPU is expected to see the biggest upside in the PCB/CCL addressable market in 
2027/28 thanks to both unit growth and content value upgrades. The next section will have a 
comprehensive analysis.  
Amazon: Trn 3’s air-cooled solution will remain at a 26L PCB design (with M8 material), while 
the liquid-cooled version will migrate to 40L. for Trn3 is solely EMC and TUC are the only CCL 
suppliers for the Trn 3. We expect to see a more significant upgrade for PCB/CCL in the next 
generation Trn 4 (higher layer counts, upgrade to M9?) 
AMD: AMD is another key growth driver for the sector, with meaningful volume into 2027/28. 
The MI450, with 48L/M8 design, will start to contribute from next year and we believe AMD’s 
MI500 series will likely adopt a 50L+ PCB design with M9 materials. This will represent a huge

increase in content value. SCC is its major source for PCB supply while CCL suppliers are EMC 
and Doosan.  
OpenAI: Our supply chain checked OpenAI’s Jalapeno roadmap and its pipeline   to reach 
1.3GW and 5GW deployment in 2027/28 is aggressive. We have limited visibility of 
Jalapeno’s hardware specs. However, given its strong performance (~700W TDP), we can 
assume it will be equipped with similar specs to TPU v8 for PCB/CCL (30-40L PCB with M8 
materials).

Google’s TPU to undergo rapid spec upgrades in next 
several years; its PCB/CCL will benefit accordingly  
The figures below illustrate the rapid production transition and specificiation advancement 
of Google’s TPU roadmap in the next several years. We highlight the following considerations: 
▶ The current V8i variant, which is undergoing HVM (through 1H28), receives a modest 
upgrade vs TPU V7 in terms of design and specification. For the latter, there are two logic dies 
and four HBM stacks encompassing TDP of approximately 1,000w. In TPU V8i, the TPD rises 
by 30% with the addition of one logic tile and two HBM stacks.  
▶ We expect the next generation of TPU V9 to undergo a massive uplift in design complexity, 
TPD and compute capability. We estimate there are 12 logic dies (4x times more than V8i) and 
12 HBM stacks (double that of V8i) to be the first to useTSMC’s N2 process node.  Given the 
rapid rise in chiplet quantity, we estimate the reticle size of the design to be 9.5x reticle size. 
The compute capability is likely to surge accordingly with the TDP of both training and 
inference to reach 4,500w, more than triple compared to TPU V8i.  We expect TPU V9 to begin 
HBM in mid-2028 and run through 2029. 
▶ We think TPU V10 will experience the next wave of significant specification and 
performance uplift as the number of logic chiplets rises further to 30, 2.5x vs TPU V9 and they 
are all to be fabicated on TSMC’s N2P geometry as well as being encompassed with 3D 
packaging for the first time. In addition, the number of HBM stacks will rise by one-third to 16 
stacks. We estimate the reticle size is likely to increase to 15x, 50% larger than TPU V9 and 
critically the TDP to surge to 10,000w if not higher!. TPU V10 is expected to enter HBM in 2029.

Implications for PCB/CCL 
We think the TPU supply chain will enjoy the biggest growth in 2027/28 along with strong unit 
growth and content value upgrades among all the GPU/ASICs. Shipments are expected to 
reach 8.5m/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. We view increasing compute power, higher HBM 
density, and a doubling of ICI bandwidth as pushing PCB designs toward significantly higher 
routing density, tighter L/S, more advanced CCL, smaller via structures, and multi-stage 
lamination to maintain signal integrity and shorten electrical paths.  
We observed that TPU’s PCB ASP has risen 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8, and 4x for the 400Gbps 
switch to 800Gbps. In addition, we estimate 2.6x growth in TPU unit demand from 2025E-27E 
and 3x expansion in the 400bps/800Gbps AI switch market by 2028 versus 2025, or 30% 
growth in total, making TAM growth exponential in these years. 
The table below illustrates the structure of Google TPU v8. One rack will contain eight 
compute trays, with each tray equipped with four TPUs, four NICs, four optical modules as 
well as two CPUs (Axiom). TOR (Top-Of-Rack)/OCS will connect those racks together to form 
a superpod (via the Virgo network) and the Juniper network to connect with other functions. 
The increasing compute power and connectivity represents significant content value growth 
for PCB/CCL based on those performance upgrades and data transmission.  
To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions lift PCB ASPs 
by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content value 
per rack for PCB is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k in 
v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given 
that TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume PCB/CCL will be further upgraded 
to meet the higher requirement.

We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventinal multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing time and lower yield rate will further drive up content value 
for PCB.

Increasing networking growth will be another pillar of growth 
ToR (Top‑of‑Rack) switch demand for AI server systems is set to grow the fastest during 2026–
28 as architectures scale-up and bandwidth requirements rise. As scale ‑up bandwidth 
increases, data move outward more quickly, shifting bottlenecks in the scale‑out network. 
Google’s TPU v8 network architecture is a good example as illustrated below.  
FIGURE 12: MLCC DEMAND IN VARIOUS AI SERVERS AND GENERAL SERVERS 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
Adoption of 800 Gbps switches is set to accelerate in 2026–27, with CCL moving from M7 to 
M8 and PCB architecture shifting from multilayer to N+M. As a result, we estimate the 800 
Gbps switch PCB ASP will rise ~275% to ~USD1,500 from USD350 –400 in 400 Gbps 
systems. The layer count will increase by 35%–40%, with the remaining value uplift driven 
by higher‑grade materials and more complex structures. For 1.6T switches, PCB ASP is

### Page 10

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL

4 October 2026  10 
expected to reach USD3,000–4,000 as the layer count expands to 44–50L and CCL upgrades 
to M9 (potentially mixed with M8 in power layers). 
At the Hot Chips Conference, a premier annual semiconductor industry event, at Stanford 
University at the end of August, Google further disclosed the networking architecture for its 
upcoming TPU8t (Zebrafish – MediaTek) and TPU8i (Sunfish – Broadcom). In addition to 
upgrading its Jupiter network (north-south traffic) architecture, which connects storage, 
general servers, and its global WAN with higher bandwidth, lower latency, and fewer hops, 
Google also detailed its new Virgo network ( east-west traffic). Through the co-design of 
network architecture, optical switching technology, and the software orchestration layer, the 
Virgo network facilitates scaling the maximum TPU cluster size to 1m units (up from 100,000 
units in TPU v7 – Ironwood).  
This upgrade highly relies on networking hardware advancements, particularly in optical 
communications. Our deep-dive research into Google’s new TPU network architecture leads 
to the conclusion that demand will increase for ethernet switches and optical transceivers.

Who will benefit most in the Google TPU supply chain?  
ISU Petasys is best positioned to capture this growth given its high exposure to networking 
PCBs (50%–60% of sales) and its role as a key PCB supplier to Google (50%+ of sales). We also 
consider EMC another great proxy for the TPU supply chain, because  of 1) its leading position 
in high-end CCL, 2) its increasing market share in ASICs (Google, AWS) as well as AMD, and 3) 
its better margin/profitability along with favorable product mix and price hike s. TTM is 
another key supplier to Google TPU for N+M products, and we expect its business to 
accelerate from 2H26. If Google confirms LTAs with its PCB/CCL vendors, we think it will 
further drive-up the supply chain’s P/E with more stable backlogs and longer order visibility.  
Price hikes will continue; LTAs are ongoing  
Another key growth driver to lift PCB/CCL makers’ profitability is price hikes. We have 
witnessed several rounds of price hikes already this year and expect more. Besides, our 
channel check suggests that CSPs are working closely with PCB/CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure advanced capacity and smooth the production process. This section will discuss these 
two issues and implications for the supply chain.  
Price hikes will likely continue  
Our supply chain checks suggest that CCL makers are increasing prices by 50%-100% YTD to 
reflect 1) organic material upgrades under the supply constraint, 2) a shortage inf upstream 
materials supply and price hikes (resin, copper foil and glass cloth), and 3) longer and more 
complex laminate process for more advanced CCL. We see price hikes across the board from 
all production lines from M8 material to M4  because tier 1 makers are allocating more 
capacity for advanced materials (which requires a longer processing time resulting in lower 
production volume). As such, entry-level products will be excluded and will partially shift to 
second-tier vendors.  
Based on current visibility, we expect more waves of price hikes in CCL in 2H26 and 1H27. We 
think each round will be 10%-15%. We assume better profitability for CCL makers in future 
quarters owing to 1) higher UTR under a supply constraint, 2) continued price hikes and 3) 
favorable product mix along with product migration.  
Are LTAs becoming new norm for PCB/CCL?  
Given the tight supply conditions and strong PCB/CCL demand for the foreseeable future, we 
are also hearing that CSPs are aggressively approaching PCB and CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure capacity. Terms will vary from vendor to vendor. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and 
LTAs for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain 
undersupplied. Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash 
flow, 2) make the supply chain more willing to invest under longer order visibility and secured 
shipment volume, and 3) improve supply chain management with better profitability. Below 
are typical LTAs between memory suppliers and their key customers.

New trends/developments in PCB supply chain and 
implications  
Given the fast development of PCB design, there is lots of noise in the supply chain about the 
future PCB demand outlook. This section will discuss the latest developments in the PCB 
industry and their implications for the supply chain, including mSAP adoption in optical, pros 
and cons for PTFE material (hybrid/pure) as well as CPO’s impact on the CCL supply chain.    
mSAP is becoming a standard for optical beyond 1.6T  
mSAP (Modified Semi-Additive Process) is not a new technology. It is an advanced PCB 
manufacturing method that builds fine circuit traces by electroplating copper onto a thin 
layer rather than etching away thick copper. It allows PCB to further shrink its L/S to below 
30um (compared to 30-50um for traditional etching). As such, PCB  can support higher 
density to support advanced electronic devices with a smaller/complex form factor.  
FIGURE 16: MSAP PROCESS IS SUITABLE FOR PCB WITH L/S <30UM  
  Standard HDI process        
L/S > 30um

Filling laser Cu plating Dry film lamination Copper etching Dry film striping 
  mSAP       
L/S <30um

Dry film lamination Filling laser Cu plating Dry film striping Flash etching 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
To meet the requirement for higher integration and miniaturization, optical modules are 
increasingly adopting mSAP to reduce product size and signal loss. Our supply chain check 
suggests that 1) we are seeing some 800G switch mSAP adoption, but it is more like an 
optional solution to ally with a specific form factor according to a vendor’s requirement, and 
2) mSAP is becoming a mandatory process from 1.6T given the L/S is expected to shrink to 
below 30um.

Implications for supply chain: The key bottleneck for mSAP is more in the 1) upstream 
materials from Japan vendors, including carrier copper foil from Mitsui Kinzoku and Nittobo’s 
low-CTE glass fabric and 2) selected equipment like laser drilling machines and laser direct 
imaging (LDI) machines. The production lead-time has increased to 10-12 months (vs the 
normal 4-6 months).  
For PCB supply, we believe SCC, ZDT and Unimicron will be the key beneficiaries  of mSAP. 
Compeq is also being qualfied for its mSAP solution for 800G/1.6T switches, based on our 
checks.   
FIGURE 18: MAJOR PLAYERS’ PROGRESS WITH MSAP

SCC is a leader in the mSAP process among Chinese PCB makers. The company 
recently announced a RMB4.37bn private placement to expand AI -related PCB 
capacity (including mSAP).

ZDT/Avary is aiming to become the world's largest mSAP supplier by expanding 
capacity 5x in the coming years at all its major production sites (including China, 
Taiwan and Thailand).  
Unimicron 
Unimicron raised its 2026 capex from TWD25bn to TWD34bn in its latest earnings

mSAP and new production site in Thailand.

Compeq has passed qualification for mSAP in high-end switch products and started 
to enter into mass production from 2H26. The company will continue to expand 
mSAP capacity in both its Taiwan and China (Chongqing) sites.

Potential PTFE developments in the future 
Nvidia is testing hybrid material (PTFE + M9) in the Rubin Ultra generation for switch trays to 
meet up with higher requirements of data transmission and may consider pure PTFE material 
for the backplane in the next generation. CCL makers are testing and sampling several 
solutions at this stage, and we will have better visibility into 1H27.

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL
