4 October 2026  15 
M9 materials based on PPO/HC resin are no longer sufficient to meet Nvidia’s performance 
requirements. PTFE has better/stable signal performance (low Dk/Df) and the current hybrid 
solution with no glass cloth can reduce the usage of Q-glass, which is under a supply 
constraint. However, the hybrid solution is difficult to process (laminate/drill), making its 
yield rate very low and higher manufacturing costs. Also, the new material will take longer 
time for qualification.  
Implication to supply chain: We don’t think PTFE will become mainstream material in AI 
servers in the near future considering its low yield rate and high cost. It is more likely to be 
adopted in areas which require extremely low Dk/Df like Nvidia is testing it for switch boards 
and potentially backplane.  
For PTFE CCL supply, we think Shengyi will be the first source given its track record with 
Nvidia. EMC/Taiflex camp will likely be the second source potentially (EMC supplies M9 PP 
and Taiflex supplies PTFE CCL). For PCB supply, we think the major suppliers will be Chinese-
based PCB vendors (SCC, Kinwong, WUS and VGT for example) and the yield rate is still low 
(below 30%).  
CPO may trigger CCL de-spec? 
CPO is an advanced heterogeneous integration of optics and silicon devices in a single form. 
It enables the integration of both PIC and EIC and packages them in a modular form factor 
along with switches or XPU (including ASIC) via substrate or interposer to support high-speed 
transmission and power saving. In traditional systems, electronic signals are converted to 
optical signals with signal loss and higher power consumption. CPO, however, places the 
optical engines closer to the switch ASIC or xPU, reducing the distance that signals need to 
travel and improving the overall performance. 
Most common data transmission involves using a transceiver module to input data into the 
switch ASIC for computing, which must pass through the PCB. For switches, the PCB distance 
between the transceiver module and the switch ASIC is approximately 11 to 12  inches or 
±300mm. This results in signal loss and reduces the switch ASIC’s signal integrity from 40 dBm 
to 20 dBm, implying a 50% signal loss. This is despite industry efforts to develop advanced 
low Df/Dk CCL to reduce signal loss and latency. As such, placing the optical engine next to 
the switch IC will efficiently resolve this problem as illustrated in figure below. 
FIGURE 20: CPO ADOPTION LIKELY TO SHRINK THE USAGE OF ADVANCED PCB/CCL

Source: Aletheia/TAG

### Page 16

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  16 
Implication for the supply chain: The CPO’s purpose is to minimize the data transmission 
on the PCB, allowing high-speed data transmission to take place within the IC level. In this 
case, PCB in switch systems may no longer require the low-Df/Dk CCL material compared to 
its use of high-end low Df/Dk CCL (M6-M8) for 400Gbps and 800Gbps switch today. As such, 
some people may be concerned that the adoption of CPO will slow down the further 
migration of CCL.  
More impact on CCL side but manageable  
As discussed above, we believe the adoption of CPO will impact the CCL side more (material 
change) instead of the PCB side. However, we don’t think it will become an immediate threat 
and the overall impact should be manageable, because 1) The design of CPO is not finalized 
and still faces challenges in higher manufacutring costs, lower yield rate, and reliability issues, 
2) Nvidia is more aggressive in CPO solutions while other vendors (like Google) still stick with 
existing solutions, and 3) the overall CCL industry is undersupplied and material upgrades of 
more and more new applications are taking place in to fulfill future demand.  
Hence, our conclusion is 1) CPO penetration takes time and the initial impact will be more in 
the Nvidia supply chain instead of the entire AI supply chain for CCL makers, and 2) the overall 
trend for CCL material upgrades will remain strong as other non-CPO application demand 
remains strong and the overall industry is still undersupplied.

More to Come 
We transfer coverage and reiterate BUY for ISU and maintain a TP at KRW270,000 on 30X 
FY27/28E PER. We remain optimistic for its accelerated revenue and earnings growth into 
2028E, driven by increasing PCB design complexity and accelerating deployment of 
Google’s TPU and expanding demand in high ‑speed networking. We believe the 
exponential growth compounded by both unit growth and content value upgrades will 
continue to lead to the growth of its AI PCB business and offset the concerns about market 
share loss. Not to mention there will be more ASIC projects to come to further boost 
growth and diversify its concentration. We view ISU as the best proxy to capture solid 
growth from both Google TPU and networking upgrades thanks to its high exposure, as 
well as leading high‑end PCB technology and strategic geographic presence in Korea. 
TPU to lead growth, but more to come  
We expect ISU’s revenue/earnings to increase sequentially from 2H26 to the entire 2027 
along with the Google TPU v8 ramp up. Despite concerns about market share loss, we view 
that 1) the absolute shipment/revenue contribution from Google will still be more than 
double, considering the TAM is growing 4-5x with higher unit shipment (3.6m to 8.5/12.0m) 
and higher content value (80%-150% higher) for both of compute and networking, and 2) less 
involvement in Google’s general server business. The company is also working on customer 
diversification; MSFT’s ASIC will become ISU’s next key growth driver from 2028 onward.  
New capacity is on the way to support future growth  
ISU is expanding capacity aggressively to meet the strong demand: 1) an earlier Fab 6 ramp 
in 1Q27 for a 25% capacity increase, 2) A 20-25% capacity increase by 2Q28 after Fab 5 
expansion for HDI to secure annual sales capacity of W2tn, according to the company. Fab 7 
is also under discussion if HDI demand comes in stronger than expected. Its core MLB (multi-
lam board) products will remain in strong demand while new HDI products are under 
development. The company doesn’t believe the increasing adoption of HDI will cannibalize 
demand for MLB. Also, we see continued price hikes (along with upstream material shortage 
and a strong end demand outlook) and better product mix will drive ISU’s profitability to 
further expand in FY27/28 under a higher UTR.  
BUY with unchanged TP of KRW270,000 
ISU is uniquely positioned to capture two major trends due to its high exposure to Google 
and networking. We project its EPS will triple from FY26 to FY28 with larger revenue scale and 
a favorable product mix. We have a BUY and an unchanged TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 
P/E. We roll over our valuation period to the average of 2027/28 with longer order visibility 
and more AI PCB projects to come, while we lower our multiple slightly from 35x to 30x to 
align with regional peers.

A Unique Proxy Standing for USA PCB 
We initiate coverage of TTM with a BUY and a TP of USD236 on a 27X FY27/28E average PER 
based on SOTP. We anticipate TTM earnings will double from FY26E to FY28E, driven by 
strong AI demand (N+M) and a solid footprint in aerospace & defense (A&D). TTM’s data 
center and networking segment will enjoy 79% CAGR sales in 2026-28 along with TPU v8 
ramping up. We believe its leading N+M position and franchise in the US will allow TTM to 
secure more AI server opportunities (like Meta) in the future . The company is also 
expanding its capacity aggressively in both Asia and the USA to capture more value in both 
AI and A&D space. 
Leading PCB maker in the US  
TTM is one of the world’s top 10 PCB makers and No.1 in the USA, as well as a total solutions 
provider for aerospace & defense (A&D) systems. We think its strong franchise in the USA and 
global footprint (capacity in North America, China and Southeast Asia) can help the company 
to seize opportunities in 1) content value upgrades in AI PCBs from Google and other ASICs, 
2) increasing spending in the A&D area and its tight relationship with the US government, and 
3) organic growth in medical and automotive spaces and continued M&A strategy.   
AI revenue to accelerate from 2H26 thanks to TPU, more projects to come  
The company reported 90% YoY growth of its data center and networking segment in 2Q26, 
and raised full-year guidance, suggesting this segment would double in 2026 with strong 
N+M demand. We project 2H26 AI-related growth will accelerate at 150%-200% YoY along 
with TPU ramping up to meet company guidance. As one of the major PCB suppliers to 
Google TPU, we see huge upside in this business: 1) TPU’s PCB ASP has lifted 2.5x from TPU 
V5 to V8 with more complex design and upgrades, and 2) we estimate TPU unit demand will 
grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 2027/28. Thanks to its strong capability in high-end 
PCBs, we believe TTM will penetrate more AI projects in the future (like Meta’s ASIC).  
EPS to double from FY26-FY28E; initiate with BUY and a USD236 TP  
We forecast TTM earnings will double from FY26E to FY28E with better product mix and 
increasing AI exposure. We expect increasing demand to accelerate capacity additions, 
supported by TTM’s leadership in high‑end PCB technology, its long‑term relationship with 
key customers and unique industry position (the largest US-based PCB maker). We initiate 
with a BUY and a target price of USD236 by using a 27x FY27/28 average PER. We use SOTP to 
derive our target multiple as we think it will better capture its dual growth drivers (AI and 
A&D), as its irreplaceability, in our view, make TTM the best proxy in the US to capture both 
AI server and national defence trends. Our 2026E EPS is largely inline with its guidance and 
consensus.

Largest PCB maker in the USA securing its industry position  
TTM is one of the world’s top 10 PCB makers and No.1 in the US, as well as a total solutions 
provider for aerospace & defense (A&D) systems. We think TTM’s strong franchise in the US 
and global footprint (capacity in North America, China and Southeast Asia) can help the 
company seize opportunities in 1) content value upgrades in AI PCB from Google and other 
ASICs, 2) increasing spending in the A&D area and its tight relationship with the US 
government, and 3) organic growth in medical and automotive spaces and continued M&A 
strategy.    
In 2025, 44% of company sales came from from A&D and 56% sales from commercials, which 
consists of data center and networking (31%), automotive (11%), and medical (14%). This 
year we started to see exponential growth from high-end AI PCB, thanks to a ramp up of N+M 
products for AI accelerators. We project data  center and networking will become the 
company’s biggest segment from this year, climbing to 49%/59%/62% of total sales in 2026-
28E.

The company has committed to invest ing over USD1.2bn from FY25-29 in advanced 
interconnect/PCB, focusing on 1) N+M products for data center and networking (AI-related 
applications), 2) ultra-HDI products for the A&D segment, and 3) R&D investment s in 
embedded optics and mSAP, for example. The company is also expanding its capacity 
aggressively in both Asia and the USA to capture more value in both AI and the A&D space.

Driver 1: advanced AI server PCB from ASIC/GPUs  
As one of the major PCB suppliers to Google TPU, we see huge upside for TTM in this business: 
1) TPU’s PCB ASP has increased 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8 with more complex design and 
upgrades, and 2) we estimate TPU unit demand will grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 
2027/28. Google’s TPU roadmap requires higher ICI bandwidth, HBM density, and compute 
power, driving a meaningful upgrade on PCB structure and ASP increases. We will elaborate 
more in the next section.

To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions increase PCB 
ASPs by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content 
value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k 
in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given that 
TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume that PCB/CCL will be further upgraded to 
meet the higher requirement.

 Multi-layer Multi-layer N+M N+M HDI HDI

N+M sequential lamination is inherently more challenging than a standard 
multilayer build because it requires multiple lamination cycles, yield rates could 
see 15-30% loss 
HDI PCB requires less layers owing to its 
high density, but it is more challenging 
than N+M because it requires multiple 
cycles of laser drilling, sequential 
buildup and ultra fine feature formation. 
That would see further yield rate loss. 
ASP Index 1 1.3 2.2 2.1 2.5 ~5-6 
Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventional multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing and lower yield rate will further drive up content value for 
PCB.  
We believe its leading N+M position and franchise in the US allows TTM secure more AI server 
opportunities in the future. Apart from Google, we reckon TTM is also one of the key suppliers 
for Nvidia switch board PCBs and upcoming LPU, which can generate incremental demand 
for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. Also, our supply chain suggests TTM is 
qualified to be one of the major PCB suppliers for Meta’s ASICs (MTIA series), which will have 
more meaningful volume in the coming years. 
Driver 2: A&D and medical segment growth from both organic and external  
Aerospace & Defense represented approximately 46% of 2025 revenue and has become one 
of TTM's most important structural growth platforms. According to the company, the current 
program backlog of A&D has reached an historical high level of USD1.7bn. TTM's A&D strategy 
is to move vertically through the electronics supply chain  from PCB/components to 
module/system solution providers as shown below. This vertical integration is strategically 
important because TTM can potentially increase its dollar content as customers move from 
individual components toward increasingly integrated electronic systems.  
FIGURE 7: TTM’S VERTICAL INTEGRATION INTO SYSTEM LEVEL

Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
TTM is investing heavily to expand North American capacity for A&D, including ultra HDI, 
munitions acceleration, defense growth, PCB reshoring and system design & integration.  
Given the sensitivity of the business, it is critical to build capacity in the US for core 
components. Management expects these investments to enable more than USD500mn of 
incremental projected throughput by 2028.

FIGURE 8: TTM’S POSITION IN A&D AREA  
Source: Company and Aletheia Capital, TAG 
Apart from organic growth, the company is also using M&A to aggressive expand its A&D 
business. In August 2026, TTM announced it would acquire Epiq, a solution provider of RF 
products, space compute, and electronic warfare applications, for approximately USD$1.1bn 
(see the press release for more details). The acquisition will elevate TTM’s vertical integration 
capabilities for its integrated electronics products and expands its addressable opportunities 
in high-priority defense mission areas. According to the company, the acquisition is expected 
to be immediately accretive to EBITDA margins and accretive to non-GAAP diluted EPS during 
2028 for the A&D business.  
Earlier this year, TTM also announced two acquisitions: Swiss Technology Group (STG) & ILFA. 
Both are European PCB companies (see the press release for more details). We view it is a 
milestone for TTM in establishing its Europe footprint. STG is a Swiss-based PCB company 
focused on medical (surgical robotics, hearing aids, medical imaging and implantable 
solutions). Its focus on miniaturized and small form-factor technology is also highly relevant 
for TTM’s A&D business. ILFA has a 45-year legacy as a German provider of complex PCB 
solutions spanning A&D, industrial, and medical technology markets.

BUY: a unique proxy standing for USA PCB  
We view TTM as a unique proxy among all PCB peers given its presence in the US with leading 
advanced capacity and tight relationships with key customers. AI PCB will lead the growth at 
a 75% CAGR in 2026-28 and boost the group sales CAGR at 39%. We forecast TTM earnings will 
double from FY26E to FY28E with better product mix and increasing AI exposure. We estimate 
ROE will expand to 26%/32% in 2026/27. 
We expect increasing demand to accelerate capacity additions, supported by TTM’s 
leadership in high‑end PCB technology, its long‑term relationship with key customers and 
unique industry position (the largest US-based PCB maker). In the meantime, the continued 
investment in high-end PCB (ultra HDI) and system integration will further secure TTM ’s 
leading position in the A&D space as one of the few key suppliers. We initiate with a BUY and 
a target price of USD236 by using a 27x FY27/28 average PER.

Elite Material 
Bloomberg 2383 TT

Market cap (USDbn) 58.2 
Average daily T/O (USD|mn) 362

Leader in CCL 
We transfer coverage and reiterate BUY and set a NT$10,000 target price for EMC, based 
on 30x FY27/28E P/E, supported by continued material upgrades and price hikes for high-
end CCL. EMC is the best proxy to seize strong upside from ASIC AI into production ramping 
up (exposure to both Google TPU and AWS Trn3) into 2H26/2027. We estimate TPU unit 
demand will grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 2027/2028. EMC’s capacity will double 
by the end of 2028 (vs ‘25) to support a strong demand pipeline and new AI CCL 
opportunities (more ASIC/GPU projects, HDI adoption and PTFE, for example) We think 
EMC’s leading position will be more secured when migrating to more advanced CCL 
materials with fewer qualified suppliers.  
Unstoppable upgrade trend in CCL  
We believe the CCL upgrade cycle will continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI 
device unit growth (especially Google TPU we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant 
content value growth via CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switches, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB/CCL to support 
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs. The overall 
industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end products, with 
the key bottleneck in upstream material (CCL, glass fibre and copper foil) to trigger further 
rounds of price hikes. We believe EMC’s leading position will become more solid when 
migrating to more advanced CCL materials (M8/M9) with 1) fewer and less qualified 
suppliers, and 2) additional capacity ready. 
Capacity to double by end of 2028 (vs 2025) to support strong growth  
EMC’s capacity will double by the end of 2028 (vs 2025) to support a strong demand pipeline 
and new AI CCL opportunities, including 1) further CCL upgrade s in TPU v9/Trn 4, 2) 
increasing adoption of HDI in AI accelerators to benefit EMC, 3) more ASIC/GPU projects like 
AMD/Meta, for example. M8 is becoming a standard material for AI servers/networking and 
we will see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). CCL is the most critical 
component in PCB and every generational upgrade implies the ASP will nearly double. 
Buy with new TP at NT$10,000 
We see a clear trend of CCL upgrades continuing into 2028 based on current design roadmaps 
and product pipelines. Its industry-leading position in high-end CCL and new capacity means 
EMC is the best proxy for upside from ASIC AI into production ramping up. We reiterate BUY 
and set a new TP at NT$10,000 with 30x FY27/28 PE. We roll over our valuation period to the 
average of 2027/28 with longer order visibility and more AI CCL upside for upgrades.

Forecast returns

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

EQUITY: TECHNOLOGY
Genius Electronic Optical   3406.TW 3406 TT
Global Markets Research
5 October 2026
EBO and CPO an ideal leverage of its capabilities 
Quantifying EBO and CPO opportunities; reiterate Buy
Action: Maintain Buy; raise TP to TWD1,415, implying ~34% upside
We expect AI optical interconnect and communication opportunities (e.g. co-packaged optics 
fiber array unit [CPO FAU], expanded beam optical [EBO], micro multi-fiber connector [MMC]) 
to transform the traditional optical lens industry over the next five years, and believe 
Taiwanese handset camera lens makers (e.g. Largan [3008 TT, Buy] and Genius) possess 
inherent advantages to cater to these new markets, given their high-precision molding+plastic 
injection, optical design, active alignment, optical coating and optical inspection capabilities, as 
well as clean-room facilities and sufficient free cash flow from cash-cow handset lens 
businesses.
In addition to holding the position of Apple’s (AAPL US, Not rated) No. 2 lens supplier, we believe 
Genius has obtained 3M’s (MMM US, Not rated) EBO ferrule order and will start shipping by end-
2026F or early-2027F. Genius appears well-positioned to supply various CPO FAU 
components (lens, prism, V groove, FA, etc.) and may potentially handle FAU assembly if 
requested by customers. We estimate the EBO and CPO-related new businesses will 
contribute <3% and 16-20% earnings for Genius in 2027F and 2028F, respectively (details 
in Fig. 10). Yet, given the high dollar content and long-term growth potential of CPO/FAU related 
products over the next five years, we believe optical lens makers with inherent advantages in this 
new field deserve stock re-rating; we thus raise our target P/E to 25x (near the high end of the 
historical range of 10-30x since 2020), from 17x. We reiterate Buy, and raise our TP to 
TWD1,415, based on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD56.61 (vs. prior TP of TWD568, based on 17x 
2025F EPS of TWD33.4, set two years ago). Genius trades at 25x 2027F P/E.
Apple: Sunny’s market share loss and continued camera upgrades in 2027-28F
Sunny (2382 HK, Neutral) earlier faced some yield issues with the new variable aperture (VA) 
main camera lens for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and this could benefit Largan (more directly) and 
Genius (indirectly) in the competitive landscape. It remains uncertain whether Genius will be 
introduced as a third lens supplier to iPhone Pro’ main camera in the future, and this poses an 
upside risk to our forecasts. However, given the headwinds of rising memory costs and the 
potential cannibalization of the 18 Pro series by the iPhone Duo, we forecast slower iPhone lens 
shipments from end-2026F to 1H27F, before the 20th anniversary iPhone (Pro Max) and the 
second-gen iPhone Duo are introduced in 2H27F. We expect further upgrades to the iPhone Pro 
main camera in 2H27F, and introduction of a new periscope camera for the Pro Max in 2H28F.

Company profile
Genius is the second source in Apple's lenses, and is gaining share in recent years. VR is a new growth engine for the company, with Facebook and
Apple being its key VR customers.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD1,415 is based on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD56.6. Our target P/E of 25x is near the high end of Genius' historical range of 10-30x since
2020. The benchmark index for this stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price

Downside risks are: 1) worse-than-expected iPhone demand, 2) rising ASP pressure due to competition, 3) worse-than-expected progress in
market-share gain in iPhone rear cam lenses, and 4) slower-than-expected penetration into other Android customers.
ESG
The company does not disclose any ESG information. However, based on industry features, we believe Genius is not a labor-intensive corporation
and it should not cause severe environmental issues.

### Page 4

Optical communication – an ideal leverage of 
lens makers’ capabilities
Optical interconnect – from MPO to MMC and EBO
Multi-fiber push-on (MPO) is the current standardized physical-contact optical 
interconnect architecture designed to aggregate high-density ribbon fibers (typically 8, 12, 
16, 24 or 32 cores per ferrule) into a single compact interface. MPO connectors leverage 
precise physical contact enforced by a high mechanical spring force (around 10-20N) and 
steel guide pins for alignment to achieve ultra-low insertion loss (0.20-0.35dB) across 
clean fiber end-faces.
The micro multi-fiber connector (MMC) is another proprietary physical-contact optical 
interconnect standard beyond MPO developed by US Conec (unlisted) for high-density 
AI interconnects to directly address the server faceplate and panel space constraints. The 
MMC introduces a very small form-factor (VSFF) multi-fiber design that utilizes a 
miniaturized TMT ferrule (patented by US Conec; Fig. 1 ), and according to US Conec, 
the width of the TMT ferrule is similar to that of conventional MT ferrule yet with ~30% 
smaller in thickness and ~50% shorter in length to shrink the physical footprint to roughly 
one-third the size of an MPO. The spatial reduction consequently unlocks up to a 3x 
density advantage in patch panel ports and transceiver faceplates compared to standard 
MPO footprints (Fig. 2 ). Meanwhile, the MMC delivers a crucial density jump while 
preserving high optical performance, offering low insertion loss and supporting up to 
250um pitch multi-fiber ribbon arrays (including 12-, 16-, 24-, and 32-fiber configurations) 
to ensure interoperability with MPO ecosystems. The MMC also improves operational 
serviceability through US Conec’s DirectConec push-pull latching mechanism to allow 
technicians to plus and unplug individual cables even at a highly populated panel without 
external retraction tools.
Fig. 1:  TMT ferrule vs. conventional MT ferrule

Source: US Conec, Nomura research
Fig. 2:  MMC has a smaller footprint than MPO to enable a 3X 
optical interconnect density

Source: US Conec, Nomura research
Although MPO and MMC are the most popular standards of optical fiber connectors 
nowadays, they both rely on direct physical contact between ferrules and thus require 
higher spring pressure and precision alignments when connecting the cables. Also, 
physical contacts leave MPO and MMC’s exposed optical interfaces inherently 
susceptible to wear and tear over repeating mating cycles as well as potential signal 
degradation from dust and debris contamination (and therefore needs frequent cleaning 
and inspection).
Expanded beam optical (EBO), a patented technology by 3M (MMM US, Not rated; 3M 
owns over 100 patents on it, with 50+ patents pending), reshapes optical interconnect 
technologies by addressing pre-mentioned challenges of MPO or MMC, since EBO 
replaces traditional physical-contact end-face matings with non-contact, lens-based 
beam expansion (Fig. 4 ). EBO utilizes integrated total internal reflection (TIR) mirrors 
within precision polymer ferrules. As the light exits an optical fiber core and enters the 
ferrule, it strikes the internal TIR mirror that collimates and expands light beams up to 10x 
their core diameter before travelling through an intentional, tiny air gap to the mating 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
4

### Page 5

connector. An identical, paired TIR mirror is integrated within the receiving ferrule to 
capture the expanded beam and focuses it back into the receiving fiber core. According to 
3M, the EBO ferrule is vapor-coated with a special anti-reflection coating that efficiently 
reduces the reflection of light beams (Fig. 5 ).
Fig. 3:  An EBO ferrule

Source: 3M, Nomura research
Fig. 4:  Connection of two EBO ferrules

Source: Rosenberger, Nomura research

Fig. 5:  A cross-section illustration of EBO

Source: 3M, EBO MSA, Nomura research

Although one of the underlying issues with EBO is a higher insertion loss (0.35-0.75dB), 
3M’s EBO connector system still provides a few advantages compared to traditional 
physical-contact connectors:
Physical-contact connectors require frequent inspection and cleaning cycle before 
mating, while EBO is nearly immune to airborne particle contamination thanks to the 
embedded lens system and no direct surface contacts. An easier handling with 
reduced maintenance efforts therefore significantly lowers the installation time (as 
much as by 85%, according to 3M) and labor cost.
•
EBO can ensure consistent signal integrity across thousands of mating cycles due 
to contact-less designs to avoid face-wear and micro-scratching. By contrast, physical 
connectors grind against each other and ultimately lacks long-term durability.
•
Traditional connectors require precise fiber-to-fiber alignments, whereas the EBO 
connector system can be used in a hermaphroditic condition (socket/connector 
agnostic) given identical connector at both ends. As such, the risk of installation 
error is largely eliminated, even without the need for fiber-optic specialists.
•
EBO only demand a fraction (e.g. one-tenth) of the high spring pressure that 
compresses glass end-faces together in physical connectors, thereby minimizing 
•
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
5

### Page 6

mechanical strains on chassis frames (demo video by 3M ). 
The contact-less, high density, blind mating, and low spring pressure features 
make EBO potentially an ideal optical connector for optical back-plane interconnect 
applications (similar to the function of orthogonal backplane or mid-plane in nVidia’s 
[NVDA US, Not rated] Kyber architecture), in our view.  
•
Fig. 6:  3M's current EBO ferrule offerings (12F/16F)

Source: 3M, Nomura research

The EBO Multi-Source Agreement (EBO MSA) was established in March 2026 to turn 
3M’s proprietary EBO technology into an open, industry-wide standard for AI cluster 
infrastructure. The primary objective is to standardize EBO connector mechanical form 
factors, latching mechanisms, and optical fiber interface dimensions to allow hyperscalers 
to source interchangeable EBO connectors from multiple vendors rather than relying on 
one single supplier. According to EBO MSA, there are 9 end-user companies (including 
Microsoft [MSFT US, Not rated], Meta [META US, Not rated], nVidia, AMD [AMD US, Not 
rated], etc.) and 48 vendors in the membership by the end of September 2026 (Fig. 7 ). 
Members vote on specific connectors to develop and contribute design specifications – 
there are 18 types under discussion, and four are on the roadmap:
128f (8x16f) design specification is underway.•
16f MPO design is under discussion on specification.•
16f VSFF and external laser small form factor pluggable (ELSFP) are planned, and 
we note that EBO development for ELSFP could be challenging since high-power 
laser could damage the AR coating and polymer ferrules.
•

Who are the potential adopters of EBO connectors?
Of course, all the key members of EBO MSA are potential users of EBO connectors. But 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
6

### Page 7

for more concrete industry developments, 3M and Microsoft announced strategic
partnership in July 2026 (link ), and Microsoft was the first announced hyperscale cloud
provider to deploy 3M EBO technology in its Azure cloud. Microsoft’s early use of EBO
technology has shown the potential to reduce network deployment timelines in certain
environments. The technology has also demonstrated strong signal performance in live
data center conditions, where dust exposure and routine handling are typical during

installation and maintenance. The technology will help Microsoft reduce the need for
frequent cleaning and inspection while supporting reliable optical performance in dense,
high-volume deployment environments.
Meta joined EBO MSA in May 2026, and from our industry checks, we think Meta is likely
evaluating EBO technology for future ASIC mass-volume optical interconnect demand.
We also observe that AWS (AMZN US, Not rated), although it is not yet an MSA member,

could potentially evaluate this technology for its future ASIC mass-volume optical
interconnect demand.
Why optical plastic lens companies have advantages in
producing ferrules for passive optical connectors?
From MPO to MMC, the fiber density increases to 3x, and we notice that the precision
level requirements for MMC TMT ferrules also increase substantially versus MPO MT
ferrules. The high precision levels (<5um, or even <3um) of MMC TMT ferrules

usually exceed the traditional optical connector makers’ plastic injection capability
(usually >10um) and traditional connector makers need to invest new, higher precision
level tools to deal with those higher precision requirements. However, those precision
levels are about the right levels (or even lower-end than) which plastic camera lens
makers can do without much new investment in machine tools. This is why we think
plastic lens makers have advantage in the advanced passive optical connectors. The

reason why we do not see many lens makers enter MMC ferrule market at this moment is
because it is a patented technology by US Conec, and US Conec only licenses to limited
suppliers, such as Sumitomo (5802 JP, Buy), etc.
For EBO as well, although EBO ferrule looks complex in design and shape, it is a suitable
product for plastic camera lens makers to do. We estimate the difficulty and price of
EBO ferrule is around the levels of mid-range handset camera lenses.

3M likely outsources EBO ferrule to Genius
From our industry survey, 3M will likely outsource the EBO ferrule components to Genius,
which is done by high-precision plastic injection. Genius can leverage its handset camera
lens equipment and make new molds to produce the product. Hence, we do not expect
too much extra capex is required.
In our understanding, fiber connector makers can produce EBO after getting authorized
by 3M. We suspect that 3M will supply the key EBO ferrule to the authorized

manufacturers at least in the early stage of technology development. This may explain
why 3M comes to reach handset lens makers for the ferrule production.
Our base assumption for Genius’ EBO ferrule revenue contribution is about 2-3%
for 2027F and 2028F, by assuming an ASP of USD0.5-1 with 20-35mn units annual
volume. We assume the GM for this new product to be higher than corporate average.
The upside of EBO market will depend on the adoption and penetration of the technology

into hyperscalers’ AI optical interconnect roadmap. By our estimate, every rack
(containing 60-80 units of GPUs or AI ASICs), if adopting EBO for optical scale-up for
intra-rack or cross-rack interconnect technology, will potentially require 600-1,000
units of EBO ferrule components (300-500 pairs). Our base volume assumption for
Genius’ EBO shipments require one major CSP to adopt EBO in one major AI
platform, plus global sampling, yield loss, trial run demand and necessary

inventory buffer. If there are more than one major AI projects adopt EBO in 2028F, it will
represent potential upside to our forecasts.
CPO opportunities could be bigger than EBO, but less clear at
this moment
Unlike that EBO ferrule is a confirmed new business for Genius (though the market TAM
upside is unclear at this moment), CPO opportunities to Genius remain uncertain in
details, such as what products to offer, what are the business models, and who are

intermediate customers and/or end customers. Not to mention, the ramp-up of CPO
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
7

### Page 8

market itself also has high uncertainties.
We believe Genius is working with FOCI (3363 TT, Not rated) in supplying key 
components for CPO FAU, such as V groove, prism, and/or fiber array (FA). It is also 
likely in talk with other FAU makers for various component supply (including lens, V 
groove, prism, FA, etc.). These FAU makers tend to do FAU final assembly in-house, and 
hence, may treat Genius as a component supplier to fill its internal capability or 
outsourcing partners’ gap. Genius’ flexibility in various component offering for CPO FAU 
could make it an easier partner for FAU customers, versus Largan (it tends to do more FA 
or FAU, rather than just components), and may see revenue contribution earlier than 
Largan. However, we assume USD25-35 ASP for Genius’ CPO FAU component offering 
in our base assumptions, given that it may supply more components rather than doing 
finished FAU products (ASP USD>100).
In the other side, we also see opportunities for Genius to get FAU module business from 
customers like Corning (GLW US, Not rated), but the progress of Genius to do such full 
assembly lines could be slower than Largan, as Genius still relies more on external 
equipment supply, unlike Largan having strong internal equipment design and 
manufacturing capability.
We assume CPO FAU to contribute Genius’ EPS by <1% in 2027F and 17% in 2028F. 
For detailed assumptions, please refer to Fig. 10 .

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, subject to 
limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an appropriate 
valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow analysis, expected 
return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated target price, defined as 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026

(target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that the 
analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 'N/A' are 
not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging Markets (ex-Asia): 
MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and by 
other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

公司拜訪快報

GIS-KY(6456) 
建議：維持中立 
結論：2026 年仍處於營運整理期

一、GIS-KY 觸控面板大廠，目前切入指紋辨識、近眼
光學業務 
GIS-KY 鴻海集團旗下觸控面板大廠，自 2011 年成立，
2012 年開始生產觸控模組 ， 成立之初是以貼合技術為核
心，結合專業的研發與製造，來提供客戶在人機介面的
整體解決方案。最大客戶為 Apple，近年除了將業務推
進至LCM 組裝外 ， 也積極發展指紋辨識 、 近眼光學業務。
2025 年主要產品營收占比，平板 61%、筆電 19%、指
紋辨識 15%、其他(車載+工控)5%。

二、2Q26 本業持續虧損，EPS-3.15 元 
GIS 2Q26 營收 105.02 億元，年減 44.8%，毛利率則從
前期 5%降至 3.3%，主要是因記憶體缺料使筆電及平板
無法拉貨導致營收大幅下滑，致使本業續虧。此外，因
母公司有資金與償還銀行借款需求，海外子公司盈餘匯
回使稅務費用提列，稅後淨損為 10.5 億元，EPS-3.15
元。 
三、未來業務主軸由人機介面轉向半導體光學 
展望下半年 ，GIS-KY 表示營收將呈現季季增的態勢 。 主
要業務觸控及顯示業務較為保守，指紋辨識業務穩定，
車載 、 光學及其他較樂觀 。 投顧預期3Q26 營收年減 17%，
主要是受記憶體短缺使終端客戶拉貨力道不足的影響，
估計 3Q26 稅後 EPS -0.1 元。整體而言，預期 2026 年
平板及 NB 業務佔比降至 70%左右，車載及指紋辨識則
約為 20%，光學及其他則有機會上升至 10%左右。 
GIS 表示未來策略重心將從傳統的人機介面，轉向「半
導體光學」(Semi-optics)，即利用半導體等級製程來製
作奈米級光學元件。其中，1)Micro-LED：用於 AR 眼鏡
的顯示，並具備短距光通訊潛力；2)SRG 光波導 
(Waveguide)：用於 AR，已在台中后里建置產線；3)車
用 AR-HUD：利用光波導技術縮小體積；4)光學鏡片：
Metalens、Pancake Lens 等，鏡頭用於高階 VR。光學
業務於 2026 年雖有專案開始量產，但營收貢獻仍小，
預計 AR 與 VR 相關應用要到 2027 年底至 2028 年，才
會開始有較顯著的營收貢獻。另外，光通訊業務主要聚
焦光源封裝，包括 CW Laser(連續波雷射)等產品，並同
步評估 Micro LED 於短距離光傳輸的應用可能性，公司
已接到美系 Coherent 的 CPO 光耦合元件 （800G/1.6T)
大單，主要應用於 AI 資料中心的高速數據傳輸，預料
光源封裝業務將在 26 年底有初步出貨，27 年上半年開
始陸續放量。為加速推動半導體光學轉型策略，GIS 今
年度資本支出預估約 80~90 億元。該筆資金將主要投
入光波導與微型發光二極體等新光學產品設備，其中有
約 40~50 億元主要用於光通訊雷射光源封裝 、 測試相關
設備。海外產能布局方面，去年 Q3 動工的越南新廠預
計於今年底完工，並力拚年底前通過客戶驗證進入量產
階段。 
四、維持中立理由 
投顧認為 GIS 目前仍以 NB 及平板觸控業務為主，隨記
憶體短缺使終端需求仍疲弱 ， 預期2026 年營收年減 15%，
營運表現未有提升，2027 年才會有明顯躍升的機會，預
期 2026 年 EPS -3.25 元，2027 年 EPS 3.28 元，維持
中立投資建議，目標價 80 元。

前 日 收 盤 價                              81 .3 0 元

GIS-KY(6456 TT)       法說 /座談摘要  
光通訊及 AR/VR 相關新事業有 望 2027 、2028 年帶來顯 著貢獻  
⚫ 營收將自 2Q26 起逐季轉佳  
⚫ 雷射封測試 量產進行中，預期年底 出貨， 2027 將有顯著 貢獻  
結論： GIS-KY 1H26 因終端零組件缺貨導致營收顯著下滑並陷入虧損，
但整體財務結構與淨現金部位依然穩健。展望 2H26，隨著記憶體短缺問
題逐步舒緩，營運預期將逐季回溫。公司目前正積極推動業務轉型，從
傳統觸控模組轉向半導體光學領域，並已擴大資本支出佈局光通訊雷射
光源封測與 AR/VR 等新產品 ， 預計這些新事業將於2027 至 2028 年成為
公司顯著的營收成長動能 。

2Q26 財務數據  
⚫ 營收 105 億元(QoQ-34.7% ，YoY-44.8%)  
⚫ 毛利率 3.3%(QoQ -1.7ppts ，YoY-3.6ppts)  
⚫ 營業利益率 -7.6%(QoQ -5.6ppts ，YoY-8.7ppts)  
⚫ 稅後淨利 -10.53 億元(QoQ-415% ，YoY-2592%)  
⚫ 單季 EPS -3.15 元 
產品營收占比  
⚫ 2Q26：平板佔 42%、筆電佔 33%、指紋辨識模組佔 14%、其他佔
11%。 
⚫ 1H26：平板佔 44%、筆電佔 32%、指紋辨識模組佔 15%、其他佔
9%。 
法說會內容摘要  
1. 2Q26 營收下滑主因為公司目前主要出貨產品以平板與筆電為主，
終端客戶受到記憶體等零組件缺貨影響而無法順利拉貨，導致營收
顯著下降。而毛利下降是因為營收縮小，導致折舊等製造與固定費
用無法獲得足夠的營收進行攤銷。  
2. 因 2Q26 進行大陸子公司盈餘匯回母公司的資金安排，須繳納相關
所得稅費用，導致最終稅後淨損達 10.53 億元，淨利率為負 10%。 
3. 公司轉型大方向維持不變，致力於從傳統的觸控顯示模組使用者介
面 (User Interface) ，轉向半導體光學相關元件發展。  
4. 公司將持續佈局 Micro LED、Waveguide、AR HUD 以及 Optical Lens
等產品線，期盼未來能有具體成果展現。

Q&A 內容摘要  
1.Q：3Q26 及 4Q26 的營運展望為何？  
A：7、8 月的單月營收約在 40 億元上下，較 2Q26 單月約 30 億元表現好，主要是記憶體缺貨
情況有稍微緩解。整體來看，3Q26 營收狀況會比 2Q26 好；而記憶體短缺的問題預計在 4Q26
會陸續得到舒緩，因此預估4Q26 營收會相對於 3Q26 更好，具體成長幅度待下次法說會詳細說
明。

2.Q：Sharp 龜山面板廠關廠對公司的影響為何？  
A：夏普是公司平板與筆電 TFT 面板的重要來源，其龜山廠在 8 月底已關廠，確實對 TFT 面板
來源有顯著影響。但終端市場正陸續轉向 OLED 面板，公司前幾年已預判此趨勢並推動轉型，
關廠只是讓此事提前發生 。 面板來源主要影響液晶顯示模組 (LCM) 後段業務 ， 但在Touch Panel
貼合業務上，即使轉用 OLED 仍可應用，受影響不大，同時公司也不排除尋找其他面板來源。

3.Q：最近 9 月初公告的資本支出用途為何？GIS 目前在光通訊領域的投資及產品是什麼？  
A：9 月初董事會核准台灣及越南子公司合計約 33 億元的資本支出，主要用於光通訊雷射光源
的封裝與測試設備，以因應終端龐大的需求。相關設備移入與實際進入量產的時間點，預估會
落在 1H27 之後。

4.Q：2026 年全年的資本支出大概為多少？項目為何？  
A：年初原本預估全年資本支出為 50 至 60 億元，但因客戶需求擴大，目前公告核准的資本支
出總額已達 80 多億元。項目包含光通訊雷射光源封測（約40 至 50 億元），以及新產品量產、
光學、AR 相關投資（約 10 至 20 億元）。這 80 億元不會集中在今年一次性現金流出，而是會
分散在今年與明年陸續發生。

5.Q：下半年是否同上半年一樣會有盈餘匯回母公司產生的稅務費用發生？  
A：公司 6 月初已公告大陸子公司有約 10 億人民幣盈餘要匯回。此金額有一大部分已於上半年
匯回，但另一部分會在下半年匯回，因此下半年的確還是會產生相關所得稅費用，不過金額不
會像上半年那麼龐大與顯著。

6.Q：越南廠目前的投資進度大概如何？ 
 A：越南廠土建即將完工，接下來將進行內部機電設備施工，預計今年底全部完工，年底到明
年初會有部分產品量產。越南廠主要生產大客戶的觸控模組，另有一部分光通訊雷射光源封裝
測試也會在該廠生產；因應物流運籌與策略考量，目前也正與其他客戶協調廠地規劃與產能。

### Page 3

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /09 /29

3 
7.Q：公司發展的新產品（包含光波導、光通訊等）目前的發展狀況為何？ 
 A：光通訊雷射光源封測的第一階段機台裝機與試量產正在進行，順利的話預計今年底前開始
出貨，明年起會有較顯著營收貢獻。光波導與AR/VR 相關產品正與客戶密切開發中，考量客戶
計畫變化與產品組合要求，預計要到 2027 年甚至 2028 年才會有較顯著的貢獻。

8.Q：關於指紋辨識產品的展望為何？ 
 A：主要提供應用於手機與平板的超聲波與電容式指紋辨識模組。中短期仍受終端零組件（記
憶體）缺貨影響出貨動能，後續若要有顯著營收成長，須期待終端缺貨狀況獲得進一步緩解。

9.Q：請問對明年展望怎麼看？新事業明年的營收貢獻預估會到哪裡？  
A：期望光通訊及 AR/VR 相關新事業在 2027、2028 年能帶來顯著貢獻。但明年具體貢獻程度
因終端市場與客戶動態變化大，可能要等到下一季季底才會比較明朗；但公司整體轉型的發展
方向不會有太大改變。

10.Q：今年在雷射光源預計的資本支出大約是 40 到 50 億元，9 月份公告的是 33 億元，是否代
表先前已有 10 到 20 億元已經投入並且即將投產？  
A：是的，去年底或今年初已陸續投入約 10 多億元於雷射光源封裝，設備已進駐並進行小量測
試與量產爬坡階段，預計 4Q26 就能產生相對的貢獻。9 月公告的 33 億元是後續再增加的擴產
資本支出。

11.Q：雷射光源封裝產能擴充後，預估 2027 年的月產能大概可以達到多少 KK？  
A：因終端市場需求非常強勁，目前公司重點在調校製程良率與生產效率，故不方便提供明確
的月產能數字，待年底將良率與效率調整到位後，再向外界做較完整的報告。

12.Q：雷射光源目前總共有幾個客戶或潛在客戶？在光通訊領域除了雷射光源外是否還有其他
機會？  
A：目前接洽對象以國際大廠為主，有些訂單已確定，有些在洽談中，不便透露具體數量或客
戶名單。期望藉由此次業務在光通訊領域站穩腳步，內部有研發多種新產品，待取得客戶信任
與認證後，會往產業鏈上下游其他相關業務發展。

13.Q：如何看待雷射光源封裝的毛利率？ 
 A：接單前內部都會評估過毛利率，達標才會接單並通過投資案。理論值雖然可以掌握，但實
際毛利率需待進入一定規模量產，產生實際營收與成本費用後才會明確；公司策略上也會優先
承接毛利率較高的高附加價值訂單。

### Page 4

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /09 /29

4 
14.Q：雷射光源封裝的 Know-how 與既有業務重疊性高嗎？主要進入門檻為設備還是人員經
驗？  
A：公司過去在電容式指紋辨識等領域已累積了長期的IC 測試與封裝經驗，並獲國際大廠認證
出貨。因此無論是人員、設備、經驗或技術 Know-how 皆具備一定實力，跨入該領域並非單一
因素門檻，而是多種經驗綜合累積的結果。

15.Q：雷射封裝測試上游原料是自行取得還是客戶指定？下游客戶是光模組廠還是CSP 廠？有
無針對特定傳輸規模（如 400G 或 800G）著墨？  
A：上游原料視不同客戶情況而定；下游客戶目前尚未直接接觸到CSP 廠，客戶商業樣態各有
不同。未來目標會以 800G 以上、較高功率與高階的晶片封裝服務來進行佈局。

16.Q：光通訊後續還有機會增加資本投入嗎？以目前 40 到 50 億的資本支出來說，兩三年回本
的機率高嗎？  
A： 期望藉由此次投資在光通訊產業鏈建立客戶信任 ，若未來評估有可行的新領域會持續跨足，
屆時就可能有增加資本投入的需求。公司當然希望能高機率回本，因此重點是提升生產良率與
效率，並穩固客戶關係，達成後回本的機率就會相對較高。

17.Q：雷射技術是以 EML 還是 CW 為主？  
A：這取決於客戶的需求，目前不管 EML 或 CW，公司的技術與能力都可以做到。

Document 286: In Praise Of Profits

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: In Praise Of Profits
Date:    2 hours ago
In Praise Of Profits

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
In Praise Of Profits
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 5 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Zac Durant / Unsplash

I. Profits, Cash Flow & Capital Spending
Corporate profits are the economy's lifeblood. As Dr. Ed explains in his
2021 book, In Praise of Profits, profits are not merely a reward for
economic success; they also drive the economy. Strong profits give
businesses both the incentive and the financial means to expand,
generating the cash flow needed to invest in capital, hire workers, and
pursue new opportunities. At the same time, profits reward successful
businesses and encourage entrepreneurs to take risks, innovate, and

### Page 2

direct capital toward its most productive uses. Consumers benefit from
employment gains and from better goods and services.
Let’s examine how Corporate America is turning record profits into record
cash flow and putting that money to work:
(1) Profits. Corporate America is highly profitable. Pre-tax profits from
current production rose to a record $4.7 trillion (saar) during Q2, while
after-tax profits reached a record $3.9 trillion (chart). That leaves

businesses with a large pool of earnings to distribute, retain, and reinvest.
Profits earned abroad by US corporations rose to a record $1.2 trillion in
Q2, confirming that the global economy is also doing well alongside the
US economy (chart). After subtracting $651 billion in profits earned in the
US by foreign corporations, net rest-of-world profits totaled $546 billion
(chart).

### Page 3

(2) Profit margins. Economy-wide and S&P 500 profit margins both rose
to record highs during Q2 (chart). The S&P 500 forward margin jumped to
18.0% in Q2, partly reflecting mark-to-market investment gains at
Alphabet and Amazon. The economy-wide GDP growth measure excludes
such capital gains and still rose to a record 11.9%.
Unit profits have risen sharply too, meaning that selling prices have
outpaced labor costs per unit of output (chart).

### Page 4

The S&P 500 forward profit margin rose to a record 16.7% in early
October, well above the S&P 400’s 8.9% and S&P 600’s 7.6% (chart). The
gap largely reflects the greater scale, pricing power, and productivity of
large-cap companies.
(3) Retained profits & cash flow. After-tax corporate profits have surged
in recent years, but dividend payments have risen much less rapidly. As a
result, undistributed profits rose to a record $1.7 trillion (saar) in Q2

(chart). That leaves businesses with plenty of internally generated funds to
pursue today’s abundant investment opportunities, not least of all those
presented by the AI boom.

### Page 5

Corporate cash flow, which combines undistributed profits with tax-
reported depreciation allowances, rose to a record $4.6 trillion in Q2
(chart). Those depreciation allowances have been boosted this year by
generous expensing provisions, including the OBBBA’s 100% write-off for
qualifying investment, which lowers current tax liabilities and boosts near-
term cash flow.
(4) Capital spending. As corporate cash flow has climbed to a record
high, businesses have been putting that money to work. Nonresidential

fixed investment has risen in lockstep and reached a record $4.7 trillion
(saar) in Q2 (chart).

### Page 6

Increasingly, that spending is being directed toward high-tech
investments. High-tech capital spending surged 16.8% y/y in Q2, far
outpacing the 2.0% gain in low-tech investment (chart).
Indeed, high-tech investment now accounts for a record 54.8% of total
nominal capital spending (chart).

### Page 7

Most of the current nonresidential investment boom is tied to AI-related
spending, particularly on software, information-processing equipment, and
R&D (chart). That is where much of the cash generated by record profits
and elevated retained earnings is now being deployed.
Profits increasingly have outpaced private sector employment (chart). This
aligns with rising productivity, especially in high-tech industries, where
businesses generate more revenue and earnings without a proportional

increase in employment. This can help offset labor shortages caused by
population aging and slower immigration.

### Page 8

The same shift is visible in the distribution of national income. Employee
compensation fell to 59.7% of national income in Q2, while corporate
profits rose to a record 17.2% (chart). Put differently, a growing share of
income is accruing to capital rather than labor. However, keep in mind that
profits are funding dividends and driving the stock market higher, thus
boosting the incomes and the net worth of many households.
II. US Economy
As Corporate America mints money and reinvests it in today’s remarkable

investment opportunities, the US economy continues to expand at a solid
pace.
The latest evidence comes from the September ISM NM-PMI. The
headline index rose to 54.9 in September, while business activity, new

### Page 9

orders, and employment all remained in expansion territory (chart). New
orders were especially strong at 59.8, suggesting continued momentum in
the service sector.
The bigger concern is prices. September's NM-PMI prices-paid index
climbed to its highest level since 2022 (chart). Historically, it has been a
useful leading indicator of core PCED inflation, the Fed’s preferred
measure of underlying inflation.
💡
Join the discussion with Dr. Ed below! Paid members can now

leave comments or questions at the end of our QuickTakes, and it
may be featured in the next "Just Ask Dr. Ed!"

### Page 10

clause.

Style Exposure
We assume coverage of Jentech at OW with a Dec-27 PT at NT$7,500, based on
30x 2028E P/E. We are positive on the company’s near-term and long-term
earnings drivers including: (1) the adoption schedule of the 3-piece / removable lid
design (removable lid + stiffener + multiple screws) could accelerate to 4Q26 with
small production in Rubin GPU and mass production from 1Q27 for Rubin/Rubin

Ultra, lifting lid content ~4–5x versus the current 1-piece design; (2) AMD MI450-
series moves to a 2-piece lid from 4Q26, driving ~10x content versus MI350; (3)
longer-cycle upside comes from CPO/NPO ramps with higher stiffener content;
and (4) potential ASIC upgrades to higher-spec solutions. As Jentech’s share price
has rallied 108% in the past three months (vs. TWSE index +102%), we believe the
above-mentioned positive factors have started to be reflected in the share price,

making our PT upside less attractive (~10%) vs. other cooling component names.
• No delay in 3-piece/removable lid adoption in Rubin Ultra, even pulling
forward some volume in Rubin potentially from 4Q26/1Q27. We continue
to believe Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU will adopt the “removable lid + stiffener
+ multiple screws” design (or the so-called 3-piece lid design) with no delay
or change, which should drive the heat spreader/lid content increase by 4-5x vs.

the current 1-piece lid design for Rubin GPU. Based on our industry checks,
Nvidia might even adopt this new design in small volumes of Rubin GPU
potentially from 4Q26 for trial production, in order to ensure decent yield when
entering mass production in the next gen (potentially in 1Q27). We believe the
adoption schedule is slightly ahead of market expectations, which might
provide near-term revenue/earnings upside for the 3-piece lid supplier.
• Solid 3Q26 and more growth into 4Q26 and 1Q27 with MI450 2-piece lid

ramp and initial removable lid contribution for Rubin/Rubin Ultra. We
estimate our 3Q26 revenue at NT$9.7bn (+33% QoQ and +91% YoY) with the
decent ramp of Rubin GPU. As the shipment of Rubin 1-piece lid should
remain at high volume into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue
momentum will also continue, with incremental growth from the mass
production of AMD MI450 2-piece lid with ~10x content increase (vs. MI350
series). Hence, we are now modeling Jentech’s 4Q26 revenue to further grow

7% QoQ. In addition, if the 3-piece/removable lid design could be adopted in
small volume of Rubin GPU from 4Q26 and enter mass production from 1Q27
in Rubin Ultra GPU, we believe there could be further upside to the company’s
growth magnitude in the next few quarters.
• Valuation re-rating already kicking off. Jentech is now trading at ~50x 12M
fwd P/E, at the high-end of its historical valuation range, but from the 2028E
EPS-based perspective, it is only trading at 25-30x P/E. With a longer-term

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component
maker worldwide, with leading production capabilities across
heat spreader products (including stiffener and all kinds of lids).
We expect the company to show an ~80% EPS CAGR over
2024-28, driven by solid heat spreader content growth in future-

gen AI chips and increasing TAM into new solutions (e.g., CPO
switch ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its
product portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU
server sockets for chip customers. We believe Jentech’s
technology leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend
and its new TAM expansion will continue to support its superior
earnings power in the long term.
Valuation
Our Dec-27 PT of NT$7,500 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which

is largely in line with Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the
AI server boom started.

Key risks to our estimates
• Potential delay in mainstream 3-piece/removable lid design adoption: As the
spec/TDP of Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU has not yet been finalized, there is still risk
of the 3-piece/removable lid adoption if the chip spec improvement turns out to be
limited. However, based on our recent industry checks, Nvidia might even adopt this
new design in small volume of Rubin GPU potentially from 4Q26 for trial
production, in order to ensure decent yield when entering mass production in the

next gen (potentially in 1Q27), which would lower the risk.
• Market share loss from competition: Based on our supply chain checks, another
Taiwanese heat spreader supplier, I-Chiun (2486 TT) has secured some CPU and
ASIC stiffener projects. However, in terms of the AI GPU lid and other higher-spec
projects (e.g CPO ASIC stiffeners), we think there is a huge gap between Jentech
and other suppliers.

Share price performance
Figure 1: Jentech’s share price vs Taiex

• In 2025, Jentech’s share price underperformed in 1H given the market concerns of
an AI bubble and potential heat spreader de-specing trend (from lid to stiffener).
However, the share price outperformed from 2H given the new high-spec heat

spreader designs (such as micro-channel lid, 2-piece lid, etc) potentially for Nvidia’s
future AI GPUs to enable TDP migration.
• In 1Q26, Jentech’s share price continued outperforming the Taiex in the beginning
with new TAM expansion opportunities from Nvidia’s new solutions, especially the
CPO related ones (e.g. Spectrum and Quantum ASICs). From the mid of 2Q26, the
share price corrected meaningfully owing to the Rubin GPU design change from 2-

piece lid back to 1-piece lid with only ⅓ of ASP.
• In 3Q26, Jentech’s share price was still weak in the beginning among the overall
market pullback from de-leveraging. However, it has rebounded rapidly from
August thanks to 1) reported GM/EPS numbers beat, 2) smooth Rubin 1-piece lid
ramp with ASP upside, and 3) more meaningful content value growth for Rubin
Ultra GPU with 4-5x ASP vs the current 1-piece lid.

Financial analysis
We forecast Jentech’s revenue to increase 61%/95%/59% YoY in 2026E/27E/28E,
supported by the removable lid design for Nvidia Rubin/Rubin Ultra GPU with 4-5x
content increase (vs current 1-piece lid), with continued heat spreader spec upgrades in
future GPUs and ASICs. Also, the more meaningful CPO/NPO ramp with higher-spec

solutions adopted also contributed to the solid growth.
Jentech’s 2Q26 gross margin expanded to 47% thanks to continued QD yield
enhancement. We believe the company’s margin will continue to surprise to the upside
in 2H26-2028, driven by the favorable product mix from stronger AI server product
shipments, which should support our gross margin forecast of 47+%/50%/52% in
2H26E/2027E/2028E. The solid GM growth also serves as the key driver of our bottom-

line EPS growth of 89%/117%/68% in 2026E/27E/28E.

J P M O R G A N
Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
Jentech Precision Industrial Co. (Overweight; Price Target: NT
$7,500.00)
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component maker worldwide, with
leading production capabilities across heat spreader products (including stiffener and all
kinds of lids). We expect the company to show a ~80% EPS CAGR over 2024-28, driven

by solid heat spreader content growth in future-gen AI chips and increasing TAM into new
solutions (e.g. CPO switch ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its product
portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU server sockets for chip customers. We
believe Jentech’s technology leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend and its new
TAM expansion will continue to support its superior earnings power in the long term.
Valuation

Our Dec-27 PT of NT$7,500 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which is largely in line with
Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the AI server boom started.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) Potential delay in mainstream 3-
piece/removable lid design adoption, and 2) market share loss from competition.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of Tommy Liou at Cathay Life Insurance Co., Ltd..

Subject: WEEKLY WEBCAST: Bond Yields On The Spectrum: From Good To Ugly (with special guest Nick Raich)

WEEKLY WEBCAST: Bond Yields On The Spectrum: From Good To Ugly (with special guest Nick Raich)

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
WEEKLY WEBCAST: Bond
Yields On The Spectrum: From
Good To Ugly (with special
guest Nick Raich)
By Ed Yardeni • 7 Oct 2026
View in browser

Is the bond selloff the Revenge of the Bond Vigilantes, i.e., a looming debt
crisis? Not yet. Despite the recent surge, the 10-year Treasury yield
remains below the growth rate of nominal GDP. That points toward an
upward repricing of the neutral interest rate. Ed and Elias examine the
spectrum of forces behind higher yields, from rising R* to a potential debt-
crisis scenario. The real warning sign would be if the yield rises above
nominal GDP growth. In addition, they consider two other spectrum
scenarios: the war in the Middle East and the yen-carry trade. … Also:
The latest labor market data suggest resilient but noninflationary strength.
🔒

latest webcast. Paid members can enjoy immediate access to the
video. This content will become available to the public at a later
date—don't miss out on early insights, consider upgrading today!

技嘉 2376 TT  ( 電子–電腦及週邊設備 ) 
AI 爆發帶動 26~27 年獲利創高 
投資與評價 
1. 3Q26 在客戶資料中心完成積極拉貨下，估營收創新高，技鋼占比逾八
成，產品結構優化使毛利率明顯跳升，獲利同創新高，EPS 11.9 元。 
2. 隨 Nebius 拉貨強勁，技嘉能見度已看到 1H27，今年產能將翻倍成
長，估營收、獲利將逐季創高，2H26 獲利 YoY+172%，EPS 23 元。 
3. 預期技鋼 27 年佔比拉升至 85%，貢獻技嘉今、明年獲利再創新高，EPS
分別達 42.2 元、51.7 元，維持強力買進評等，上修目標價至 535 元。
(以稀釋後 26 年獲利估 13.5x 評價)  
重點更新 
3Q26 營收、獲利將再創高 
估技嘉 3Q26 營收 1,587 億元，QoQ+10%，YoY+100%，主要因客戶資料
中心完成積極拉貨硬體，且本季高階顯卡銷售強勁。估技鋼占比逾八成，其
中 GB 比重約 20~25%，在產品組合優化且部分低價庫存貢獻，加上消費產
品銷售回穩，估毛利率將回升至 11.4%，營業利益 YoY+245%至 118.6 億
元，上修稅後獲利 20%至 EPS 11.9 元，獲利將再創歷史新高。 
技鋼在手訂單能見度已看到 1H27 
AI 在手訂單已達 1H27，較過往一季明顯提升，主因 Nebius 硬體集中於
2H26 進駐，客戶大型合約須確保供應鏈產能，加上主權 AI 貢獻，預期
2H26 技嘉營收獲利皆翻倍成長，且營收增幅延續到 1H27，上修 4Q26 營
收至 1,742 億元，YoY+95%，Vera Rubin 將有完整貢獻，機櫃比重估三成
以上。此外，高端顯卡通路售價遠高於 MSRP，預期消費產品營收季增，毛
利率小幅回落下，EPS 12.5 元，獲利再創新高。2H26 上修獲利 21%至
163.7 億元，YoY+172%，EPS 23 元(已考量 ECB 對股本膨脹 5.97%影響) 
技嘉隨客戶 Nebius 2H26~1H27 DC 建置完成而營運增速 
Nebius 10 月全面調漲租賃價格，其中，Nvidia Blackwell 上調 19~21%，
Hooper 上調 17~20%，隨 1H27 客戶數個新資料中心落成，且繼續交付
Hyperscaler 訂單，最大供應商技嘉今年產能翻倍成長，受惠訂單強勁且確
認度提升，上修技嘉 2026 年、2027 年營收至 YoY+73%、37%，技鋼分別
年增 120%、46%，27 年佔比拉升至 85%，更多垂直整合及 GADU 加入下，
獲利持續創高，上修今、明年獲利 12%、16%至 EPS 42.16 元、51.73 元。

### Page 2

圖一:Nebius 自 10/1 調漲價格 19~21%

資料來源:Nebius，國泰證期研究部。

公司簡介 
技嘉科技(2376 TT)創立於 1986 年，以自有品牌 Gigabyte 行銷全球，主要產品包括主機板及顯
示卡，為全球前三大的板卡廠，同步發展電競筆電業務，技嘉擁有電競品牌 AORUS，另有創作
者系列筆電 Aero，持續提升高階 NB 市占。旗下技鋼主營伺服器業務，AI 佔比達九成，目前為
公司最主要成長來源。技嘉營收比重為消費產品22%，商用產品 78%。地區占比為 ASIA 58%、
America 17%、Europe 25%。

### Page 3

ESG 政策 
國泰給予技嘉 ESG 評等維持 AA，因其在環境系統與治理等構面之資訊揭露更為完備，且
資料中心產品導向以高效能、低能耗之產品為主推。優異之處在於環境系統與治理、ESG
創新、人權、多元組成與包容性。總體來說，公司在 CSR 揭露較電子同業完善，惟建議產
品製程計畫及應變流程中，可進一步揭露員工產品品質及教育訓練投入時間及成本，以利
有效掌握該構面表現及追蹤後續成效。

⚫ 供應鏈社會面向控管 (SASB 重大性議題，電子業排行 6%) 
技嘉制定 《技嘉科技永續採購準則》 ，企業社會責任面向中涵蓋勞動人權、衝突礦產、企業
道德等子目標，並訂有四項零容忍規範，包括禁童工、禁強迫勞工、禁排放未處理有害物
質及立刻造成員工身體傷害之行為。供應商稽核方面，技嘉將既有及新進供應商進行分級
管理，評比內容除了供貨合約與物料評比外，亦納入永續供應商評鑑制度，每季進行供應
商 CSR 高風險評核，初步淘汰高風險 D 級供應商，確保供應鏈的品質穩定性。此外，
2022 年技嘉亦遵循負責任商業聯盟準則(RBA)， 評鑑供應商在企業社會責任面向之表現 ，
整體平均分數達 93.5 分。衝突礦產方面，技嘉導入責任礦產計畫，調查供應商衝突礦產
使用情況，要求供應商制定明確衝突礦產管理政策，22 年技嘉採購金額前百大供應商衝
突礦產管理執行率為 73%，並持續提供輔導，以 100%為管理執行率目標。

⚫ 供應商及產品生命週期管理 (SASB 重大性議題，電子業排行 7%) 
技嘉制定 《技嘉科技永續採購準則》 ，主動採購具環保標章、能源標章、省水標章、綠建材
等全球相關綠色標章認證產品，或其製造、使用過程、廢棄處理對環境資源耗用較少、低
碳、污染程度較低之產品外，亦將符合 ISO 14001 第三方認證之環境管理體系或自行建
構獨立運作環境管理體系之供應商納入優先採購名單，並要求供應商進行環保及品質管制
並提供相關 RoHS 檢驗和測試報告，確保符合 HSF 管制要求。此外，技嘉亦依循「供應
商品質管理作業規範」根據合作狀況按月及按季評比，融入永續供應商評鑑制度，針對表
現不好者提供輔導或汰換處理，遴選出優良廠商頒發永續廠商獎，促進廠商在永續績效上
形成良性競爭共 82.21%的供應商參與永續性整合評鑑。供應商輔導方面，技嘉制定減量
333 計畫，鼓勵供應商以每年減水、碳、廢棄物各 3%為目標，藉由供應商大會作為廠商
間交流永續作為的平台外，提供實務訓練進行輔導，22 年共 130 家供應商響應計畫，其
中 55 家提供量化績效。產品生命週期管理方面，技嘉導入碳足跡及水足跡盤查，評估產
品對環境衝擊。

### Page 4

原物料與廢棄物管理 (SASB 重大性議題，電子業排行 7%)  
技嘉於永續報告書揭露當年度原物料使用量，並透過精簡包裝、提高可回收材料比例及採
用保麗龍回收料等方式，降低包材物料使用量。技嘉自 2011 年開始分析包材回收率與成
分，透過筆電研發設計部門進行筆電包材材積優化改良，經推估不同系列產品材積減少  
19-52% 不等，產品包材的總重量較 2011 年減少 60.43%，持續以 2030 年起不使用一
次性包裝材做為最終目標。

廢棄物管理方面，技嘉制定廢棄物管理政策，並揭露一般及有害事業廢棄物和回收總量。
技嘉亦制定具體減量目標，以持續追蹤廢棄物管理績效，目標每年減量達3%，並於 2030
年達成較 2010 年減量 50%目標。針對電子廢棄物，技嘉透過子公司參照荷蘭環境評估署
定義的 9R 循環經濟主張，進行評估分析後進行維修及整新，2022 年共為 660,710 件商
品延續生命，相當於減少 774.2 公噸的電子廢棄物。

有害物質管理方面，技嘉建置有害物質管制規範，區分禁用物質、限期禁用物質及未來潛
在禁用物質三級有害物質，系統性管理有害物質清單，並針對危險性即時應對。此外，公
司亦於每季綠色永續發展委員會中呈報國際法規變動情形，使技嘉可即時針對有害物質使
用的國際趨勢進行調整。

ESG 創新 (SASB 重大性議題，電子業排行 9%)  
技嘉設置綠色永續發展委員會，由董事長擔任主席，1~2 個月召開會議討論永續、環境或
產品相關議題。綠色認證方面，技嘉成立子公司百事益，建立永續循環經濟商業模式，百
事益於 22 年取得 BS 8001 循環經濟標準、商業模式成熟度最佳化認證，榮獲第三屆台灣
循環經濟獎的創新服務獎傑出獎及 SGS 品質卓越案例獎。綠色設計方面，技嘉針對產品
進行高效率低耗能的設計與改良，透過於產品生命週期中導入各式創新材料、技術和管理
機制，促成產品高效能與耐用的表現。技嘉亦因應資料中心需求贈加，自 21 年開始協助
客戶建置不同浸沒式冷卻運算機房，有效降低 90%耗能。

智慧生產方面，技嘉亦提出倉儲物流自動化解決方案，導入無人車使生產過程更加效率及
安全，且在疫情期間成功減緩人力短缺問題。智慧財產管理方面，技嘉制訂《技嘉集團智
財權綱領》 及 《技嘉集團專利管理辦法》 ，以擬定智慧財產策略，並保護關鍵技術。此外，
亦透過「技嘉科技集團專利獎勵辦法」 、辦理教育訓練與提案競賽，激發內部員工創新意
識，累積至 22 年已取得 2832 項專利，並有 104 人獲頒內部專利獎勵。

⚫ ESG 創新 (SASB 重大性議題 電子業排行 9%) 
技嘉成立隸屬於總裁室之綠色永續發展委員會，作為永續發展事務推動的統籌決策和運作
組織委員會職責為氣候治理策略、價值鏈合作及具體減緩與調適計畫，每個月至少召開一
次會議，由各組織代表會報永續、環境或產品法規與趨勢，提出公司應對策略以求即時因
應國際脈動。製程創新方面，技嘉執行 19 項製程智能化，包含產線配置與流程優化、設
備改善開發、作業輔具導入等 ，將智能系統技術運用於生產過程中。為促進循環經濟，技
嘉 2018 年成立百事益國際 ， 透過 「以租代買」 商業模式 、 整新品再售 、 開拓過季品市場 ，
推動「全球甜檸檬認證標章」的逆物流整合服務，以期解決買賣雙方資訊不對稱導致低價
劣品充斥的「酸檸檬市場」 。法律遵循方面，近 3 年未意圖扭曲市場正常運作而受公平交
易委員會懲罰，未與供應商訴訟情事產生。

### Page 5

ESG 可加強處 
產品安全 (電子業排行 83%)  
產品安全方面，技嘉推行全面品質管理，以達成環境保護與客戶滿意，品質政策包括 ISO 
9001／IECQ QC 080000， 其中 ， 技嘉符合IATF 16949：2016 汽車業品質管理系統和 ISO 
9001 的品質管理等相關認證。惟建議技嘉可以透過自行設立實驗室或委託第三方公正實
驗室抽樣產品品質，以確保產品品質。此外，亦建議公司揭露員工產品品質方面之教育訓
練具體投入時間或費用，以利利害關係人瞭解公司於提升產品品質之投入。

風險及危機管理 (SASB 重大性議題，電子業排行 72%)  
風險及危機管理方面，技嘉依循風險控管流程鑑別出十大面向風險，並評估衝擊程度及發
生機率後繪製成風險矩陣，針對各類風險訂定管控政策，惟建議技嘉可以制定具體稅務治
理政策，以降低各項營運產生之稅賦成本。

資訊安全方面，技嘉設置資訊安全委員會，由總經理擔任最高管理層級，在資訊管理政策
上遵循國際資安標準(NIST CSF 框架)及國內外法規。惟建議技嘉導入 ISO 27001 相關資
訊安全管理系統，並取得第三方認證，且建議公司針對資訊或網路安全至少每年一次定期
進行檢視或測試，及說明最近一次評估結果，。此外，建議技嘉可以揭露員工資訊安全相
關教育訓練之訓練時數或完訓率，以利利害關係人瞭解公司於維護資訊安全之投入。

### Page 1

十月 01, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒將為 N3 需求挹注新動能 
重要訊息 
台積電將於 10 月 15 日召開 3Q26 法說會。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 財報可望略優於財測。 在強勁的算力需求與 iPhone 備貨旺季帶動下，
凱基預估台積電 3Q26 營收將季增 16.2%至 1.48 兆元，略優於公司財測高
標。由於 7-8 月營收已達成凱基 3Q26 預估值的 67%，故 9 月營收可能較 8
月略為下滑，我們亦預估 3Q26 毛利率將達 68% (財測區間為 65-67%)，係
高產能利用率與產品組合改善抵消 N2 量產及海外設廠費用對毛利率的稀釋
效應；我們預估第四季營收季增率為高個位數，毛利率則可能與 3Q26 持平
或略微下降，因 N2 業務佔比擴大影響 ，凱基預估毛利率為 67.6%。 
產能吃緊有助推升產品價格。 凱基預估 2027 年台積電先進製程價格將調漲
5-10%，有別於 2026 年僅調漲 N3、N4 與 N5 的價格，我們認為在 HBM4
基底晶粒 (N12 製程) 新需求與 CPU 需求強勁  (I/O 使用 N6 與 N7 製程) 的驅
動下， 2027 年所有先進製程價格都 將調漲。先進封裝與成熟製程的價格可
能分別調漲 10-15%及 3-5%。此外，隨 Nvidia Rubin 晶片量產爬升、強勁
AI ASIC 需求及 Agentic AI 帶來的 CPU 與網通晶片需求升溫下 ，我們預期
2027 年 N3 產能將維持吃緊 (產能利用率 120%以上)，並預期在台灣、美國
與日本廠產能開出下，2026- 28 年底 N3 月產能可達 16/20/22 萬片。凱基預
估台積電 2027 年營收將年增 32.8%，資本支出介於 700-720 億美元。  
HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒將為 N3 需求挹注新動能。 台積電日前宣佈將再加
碼擴產 N3 三座廠。由於目前 N3 產能已達過往單一製程節點的 甜蜜點(約每
月 15 萬片晶圓)，持續的積極擴張在單一製程是過去罕見的。有鑑於 N3 的
兩大客戶將於 2028 年改採 N2 (Apple) 及 A16 (Nvidia) 製程，我們認為 其積
極擴充 N3 產能的背後原因是 HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒。假設 GPU 出貨量達
1 千萬顆且皆採用 HBM4e，則所需的 N3 晶圓可能高達 55-60 萬片，換算單
月需求量為 4.5-5 萬片，相當於台積電 N3 產能的 20-25%。此外，我們亦
預估 2027-28 年 AI ASIC 與 CPU 需求將維持強勁，故即使台積電進一步擴
充產能，至 2028 年底前 N3 的產能利用率仍維持在高檔。 
投資建議  
凱基維持台積電「增加持股」評等，目標價 3200 元，相當於 2H27-1H28
平均每股盈餘的 20 倍。台積電依舊是我們在半導體領域的首選個股。  
投資風險  
全球景氣不確定性；積極擴產導致供給過剩。

圖 3：公司概況  圖 4：台積電各製程晶圓營收比重  
台積電是全球最大的專 業半導體晶圓代工廠， 近年來其市占率
持續有所增加。 2013 年的八吋約當產能超越一千六百萬片。
台積電現有六座先進十二吋晶圓廠、六座八吋廠、一座六吋
廠，外加正在興建的一座十二吋廠。台積電提供以下服務： 晶
圓製造，晶圓探測，組裝和測試，光罩生產和設計服務。

台灣 台積電

十月 01, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以8，係假設1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

### Page 1

九月 30, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  消費品 ‧ 台灣

八方雲集 
(2753.TW /2753 TT)

美國市場展店進度如期 
重要訊息 
我們參加八方雲集法說會。管理層表示台灣市場仍具進一步展店空間，並導
入全新外帶 TOGO 店型以拓展更多據點。在美國市場，公司 上週於德州開設
首家門市，採用全新快 餐營運模式，我們認為有助加速美國業務轉虧為盈。  
評論及分析  
持續展店。今年迄今公司旗下兩大主力品牌八方雲集與梁社漢排骨台灣市場
分別淨增加 25 家及 12 家門市。受惠來客數與客單價同步提升，兩品牌今年
以來同店銷售分別成長 5%及 6%，管理層認為台灣市場仍具展店空間。公
司正推動全新的外帶店型， 相較於傳統內用門市，新 店型可降低前期資本支
出，並有助進入 高人流但空間受限、不易設置內用座位的據點。考量目前外
帶比重已高，我們認為外帶店不僅有助擴大客群覆蓋範圍，亦可提升加盟主
獲利能力，進一步支撐公司長期成長動能。  
美國市場導入新店型。八方雲集上周 在德州開設首家門市，並導入全新的快
餐店型，提供較為精簡的套餐組合。管理層表示新店開幕後三日，套餐產品
佔營收比重已達 70%，大幅縮短店內備餐時間，並有助降低人事成本。公
司預計今年底前德州再 新增兩家門市，將 美國總店數提升至 19 家，較去年
增加六家。公司目前已簽約五處新據點，計劃明年進軍亞利桑那州市場，目
標 2027 年新增 6-8 家門市。我們預估美國市場營收佔 比將由今年的 10%，
進一步提升至 2027 年的 14%及 2028 年的 18%。我們認為新店型將 有助
加速美國事業轉虧為盈。  
獲利穩健成長，股利 配發穩定。儘管原物料成本影響 2Q26 毛利率，管理層
預期豬肉及雞肉價格漲勢不會持續， 並預期 2H26 毛利率可 持穩於目前水
準。我們根據最新展店計畫及美國業務損平時點 ，調整獲利預估。受惠營運
現金流持續成 長，我們預期公司將維持穩健的現金股利政策， 過去五年平均
配發率為 82%，預估未來兩年現金殖利率為 6-8%。 
投資建議  
受惠同店營收成長及展店規模擴大，我們認為八方雲集台灣市場利潤率 將持
續擴張。隨著美國市場 進一步展店，我們預期美國營運 2027 年將轉虧為
盈。我們維持「增加持股」評等及目標價 234 元 (16 倍本益比)。 
投資風險  
展店進度延宕與原物料價格上漲。  
增加持股‧維持

年 8 月在台灣、香港及美國共有 1,412 家門市。旗艦品牌八方
雲集為營收大宗，營 收佔比 約 75%，其次是 梁社漢排骨 為
24.8%。公司大多數的門市都採加盟形式經營。 2Q26 台灣市
場貢獻營收 77.7%，香港佔比為 12.7%，其餘則來自美國市
場。

### Page 1

九月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  電子零組件 ‧ 台灣

勤誠 
(8210.TW/8210 TT)

短期營收與獲利表現疲弱 
重要訊息 
7-8 月營收 49.1 億元，低於我們預估，因 ASIC 伺服器機殼拉貨不如預期。
我們預期 3Q-4Q26 營收及利潤率將低於我們 原估。 
評論及分析  
凱基下修 3Q26 獲利預估。 7-8 月營收合計 49.1 億元，僅達我們 3Q26 營收
原估之 58%，因 ASIC 及 HGX 營收較預期疲弱。根據 供應鏈訪查，AWS  (美) 
Trainium 3 (T3) PDS 機櫃月組裝量低於 ODM 廠原先目標，主因機櫃外殼良
率較低。因勤誠機殼 2Q26 已被提前拉貨，使近期 ASIC 機殼出貨低於我們
預期。 此外，3Q26 HGX 營收恐 季減， 弱於原估之微幅季增。我們下修
3Q26 整體營收至 季減 4%。因產品組合較不利 (HGX 營收比重下滑 )、NPI
專案較少，下修 3Q26 毛利率至 29%，下修 EPS 至 9.33 元，季減 16%。 
4Q26 營收可望季增，但仍低於凱基原估。 我們預期 4Q26 T3機櫃組裝逐步改
善，帶動勤誠 ASIC 機殼營收恢復季增，但仍低於原預期。儘管 VR200 機殼也
將量產，但我們下修 ASIC 與 HGX 營收，以及新一代降噪櫃遞延使機櫃營收
低於預期，4Q26 營收下修至季增 12%。考量 ASIC 及 VR200 機殼營收比重提
升使產品組合較不利，下修 4Q26 毛利率至 29%。我們下調 4Q26 EPS 預估至
10.59 元，季增 14%，並下修 2026 年 EPS預估至 41.75 元，年增 44%。 
2027 年營收成長，但毛利率年減 。2027 年 CSP 客戶對 AI 伺服器需求仍成
長，勤誠主要營收成長動能包括：(1) ASIC 伺服器機殼放量；(2) 受惠市占率
提升，Nvidia ( 美) GB300/VR200 伺服器機殼營收 成長；及  (3) AMD ( 美) 
Helios 機櫃、CDU 專案及客製化機櫃出貨。然而，我們下修 HGX 與降噪櫃營
收，2027 年整體營收下修至年增 34%。加上產品組合較不利  (ASIC 與機櫃
比重提高)，以及新產能投產使折舊費用增加，我們認為 2027 年毛利率與營
利率年減幅度將大於我們原估，下修 2027 年 EPS 至 52.76 元，年增 26%。 
投資建議  
短期 AWS ASIC 與 HGX 拉貨放緩將拖累勤誠 3Q-4Q26 營收與獲利，考量勤誠
AWS營收比重較高 (2026年比重 60%-65%)，且短期動能有限，調降投資評等
至「持有」。我們預期 2027年毛利率年減幅度較大且較難回到過往高峰，下修
目標本益比以及目標價至 950元，基於 18x 2027F EPS (原25倍)。 
投資風險  
伺服器需求低迷、新專案遞延、總體經濟不確定性 。 
持有‧調降

焦點內容

2. 我們預期 3Q-4Q26 營收與利潤率表現疲
軟。 
3. 2027 年客戶 AI 伺服器需求仍成長，但產
品組合較不利將使勤誠毛利率年減。

圖 5: 公司概況  圖 6: 1H26 美系客戶營收比重 提升至 69% 
勤誠興業創立於 1983 年，為機櫃式、塔式伺服器及個人電腦
機構方案設計製造商。 2025 年客戶別營收比重則為美國客戶
60%、中國客戶 30%、歐洲客戶 7%、其他地區 3%。勤誠近
年成功擴展業務至資料中心與工業解決方案，並持續提高市佔
率以及提升營收。

### Page 1

十月 01, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點摘要

泰合 
(6467 TT, NT$ 230, 未評等)

重點摘要  
1. 泰合以口服膜劑型平台為核心，主力產品 TAH3311 為全球首款
Apixaban （原廠 BMS Eliquis ）口服膜，鎖定吞嚥困難的高齡抗凝
血病患。美國以 505(b)(2) 途徑申請， PDUFA 目標日為
2027/4/22 ，將先取得 Tentative Approval ；歐洲已於 10M25 送
件，預計 2026 年底取證。生產委託德國 LTS 代工，兩條線合計
年產能約 5 億片（5mg），每片生產成本不高於 0.5 美元 
2. 近 期 里 程 碑 依 序 為 ：BMS 是 否 提 告 （45 天 期 限 ， 公 司 預 期
10M26 下旬揭曉）、紐澳首件授權（公司表示 2026 年內一定簽
署），以及歐洲授權（最快 2H26-1H27 ）。授權採雙軌並行，同
時與 BMS 及非原廠洽談，全球已與逾 30 家藥廠簽署保密協定；
若與 BMS 合作可提早於美國上市，非原廠路線最差約 11M28 與
學名藥同步上 市 
3. Eliquis 2025 年美國銷售額約 292 億美元、年增約 12%，公司估
美國吞嚥困難 AF 患者目標市場約 110-164 億美元。泰合自持藥
證與供貨，收益涵蓋簽約金、里程碑金、 10-30% 分潤及出廠價
與代工成本價差。我們認為，自持藥證使泰合在夥伴選擇與定價
上保有主導權，加上定價以原廠為錨、不與學名藥比價，有助於
在 2028 年學名藥進入後維持單位獲利  
結論 
我們認為 TAH3311 已進入取證前的關鍵窗口，美歐法規時程明確，後
續評價重估的關鍵在於授權能否落地，而 10M26 下旬 BMS 是否提告
將決定美國合作對象與上市時點 。我們看好公司以自持藥證、代工價
差加分潤建立的「絕對值」收益模式，較一次性簽約金更能反映產品
長期銷售潛力；惟紐澳授權已較原規劃延遲、歐洲為避免影響美國定
價而放慢節奏，且現有產能僅約美國需求的 1/6 。短期首要驗證點為
2026 年內紐澳授權簽署與 BMS 的回應，中期則需觀察擴產資金與時
程，以及 FDA 審查團隊更替對審查進度的影響

TAH3311 產品定位與 Eliquis 市場 
 TAH3311 為全球首款 Apixaban 口服膜，一天兩片，適應症涵蓋
AF、DVT、PE 及髖膝關節置換術後；泰合自行完成歐美三期並自持
藥證 
 Eliquis 2025 年美國銷售額約 292 億美元，年增約 12%，公司稱為
全 球 最 大 小 分 子 藥 ， 同 機 制 第 二 名 約80 億 美 元 ；BMS 1H26 
Eliquis 營收年增約 20%（1Q26 約 16%） 
 受 IRA 降價影響，單顆價格由約 8-10 美元降至 3.85 美元，公司認
為降價後用藥人數增加，新處方轉向 Eliquis ；出血風險 0.66% 是其
獨大主因，惟與約 380 種藥物有交互作用

凱基投顧

吞嚥困難族群與目標市場  
 美國 2025 年 AF 患者約 1,055 萬人，其中 75%為 65 歲以上，高
齡者中 50-75% 有吞嚥困難；公司估 Eliquis 在 AF 的滲透率約三
成，美國 AF 患者約四成以上有吞嚥困難  
 主要共病的吞嚥困難比例：中風約 50%、失智症約 68%、帕金森
等運動神經疾病約 84% 
 以月藥費 231 美元（一天 3.85 美元）×12 個月計，美國目標市場
約 110-164 億美元 
 保險方角度：美國每年約 45 萬名 AF 患者住院，每人約 4.67 萬美
元、合計約 211 億美元；相較之下穩定服藥一年約 2,772 美元

法規時程與專利  
 美國以 505(b)(2) 申請，PDUFA 目標日 2027/4/22 先取得 Tentative 
Approval ，待訴訟結果再取得 Final Approval ；公司以中小企業資
格申請，首個產品免申請費（約 240 萬美元） 
 歐洲於 10M25 送件，預計 2026 年底取證 
 專利時程：  
 BMS 美國物質專利 2026/11/21 到期，加計兒童資料 6 個月延至
2027/5/21 ；劑型專利 2031/4 到期，加 6 個月延至 2031/10  
 美國以外物質專利 11M26 到期，歐洲將有學名藥進入  
 美國學名藥和解上市最快 10-11M28 ，約 4 家 
 泰合自有專利保護至 2042 年

BMS 訴訟進展  
 FDA 受理後 20 天內已通知 BMS，並附非侵權說明與樣品； BMS 有
45 天決定是否提告，公司預期 10M26 下旬揭曉  
 非侵權主要依據為溶出率差異：原廠 30 分鐘溶出率高於特定值，
泰合則低於該值  
 前例方面， BMS 與學名藥廠官司約歷時 15-29 個月，與漢達口溶錠
案約 7 個月結束；公司認為新劑型案不會拖太久，並視訴訟為談判
起點，BMS 若不提告即可直接洽談授權

授權策略與收益模式  
 採雙軌策略， 同時洽談 BMS 與非原廠，並依國家獨立授權，先美
國以外、再回到美國；全球已與逾 30 家藥廠簽署保密協定。非原
廠對象為年營收 10-100 億美元藥廠，且須能達到 Destum 估的
peak 市佔 9% 
 各區進度：  
 紐澳：首個授權對象年營收約 20-50 億美元，公司表示 2026 年
內一定簽署，簽約金不會太大

### Page 3

台灣 泰合

十月 01, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

 歐洲：最快 2H26-1H27 。因美國比價政策，公司擔心歐洲過早
低價授權影響美國定價，考慮先自行核價（美國約 3-6 個月、歐
洲約 6-9 個月）；德國價格接近美國  
 美國：待 10M26 底 BMS 態度再定。與 BMS 合作無專利問題可
提 早 上 市； 非 原 廠 路 線 最 快 於 物 質 專 利 到 期 後 上 市 ， 最 差 約
11M28  
 BMS 可能以高價切入並逐步替換市場，非原廠則可能以較低價搶
市；公司稱 BMS 年營收約 480 億美元，此產品可為其增加約三成
營收 
 定價以原廠為錨、不與學名藥比價；原廠轉向直銷後每顆仍可賣 4-
5 美元，高於 Medicare 標價 3.85 美元 
 收益看「絕對值」 ：出廠價與代工成本價差、簽約金、里程碑金及
10-30% 分潤合計；合約通常十年並設銷售門檻，未達可另覓第二
家夥伴

代工產能與成本  
 代工廠為德國 LTS，公司稱為全球最大口膜貼片廠，已簽長期合約  
 每批 480 公斤，可切 160 萬片（5mg）或 320 萬片（2.5mg ） ；一
條專屬線年產能約 2.5 億片，另一條同規模線使用率低可調用，合
計約 5 億片（5mg） 
 公司估美國需求約 30 億片／年，現有產能約 1/6；擴產僅需補設
備，下單至安裝約 6 個月，再跑 3 批並送安定性資料，已查過的廠
不需重新查廠；擴充時泰合需分攤部分設備費用  
 每片生產成本不高於 0.5 美元、可能更低；除分潤外，泰合對夥伴
的出廠價另有加價。對照原廠毛利率約 80-90%

商業模式與產品線  
 多數產品開發至 Phase 1 後對外授權，由夥伴執行三期與取證，泰
合收取簽約金、里程碑金與分潤； Apixaban 因市場規模大，改為
自行做到三期並自持藥證，夥伴銷售不佳時可更換  
 長 效 劑 型 TAH3341 （ 一 天 一 片 ） 尚 未 進 入 臨 床 ， 初 步 規 劃 與
TAH3311 一同授權。若 FDA 僅看血中濃度則較簡單，若要求證明
較少出血或中風，試驗可能需約 1.5 萬人、3 年；FDA 原審查團隊
已全數更換並曾發出補件要求，公司暫不主動對外說明  
 其他產品線：  
 Naloxone 口頰溶膜：鴉片類（如芬太尼）過量急救，約 2-3 分
鐘起效 
 Nintedanib 口頰溶膜：特發性肺纖維化， 原廠膠囊 BA 約 4.7%
且有嚴重腹瀉，目標將 BA 提高數倍 
 Ondansetron 舌上口膜：化療嘔吐止吐，已於日本取證  
 另有 ADHD 口服液／貼片（小兒過動症）與 Donepezil 貼片（阿
茲海默症）

台灣 泰合

 CDMO ：已為美國兩家藥廠完成劑型開發，第三家進行中；上市後
目標建立自有生產線（台灣目前無口膜廠）

Factor XIa 競爭 
 FXIa 位於凝血路徑較上游，訴求出血風險更低；大分子注射型僅適
合高出血風險小眾，不利長期用藥  
 Milvexian （BMS 、Janssen ）於急性冠狀動脈症候群三期失敗； AF
三期約 1.55 萬人、以不劣於 Apixaban 為目標，結果預計 2027 年
公布。劑量約 100mg ，為 Apixaban 5mg 的 20 倍，錠劑更大、更
難吞嚥 
 公司認為高價的 FXIa 僅能搶攻高出血風險族群（腸道出血、腎功能
不佳、癌症等） ，短期不影響TAH3311 定位與 BMS 談判

QA 
 紐澳授權何時簽署？為何延遲？  
 公司表示 2026 年內一定會簽，但不確定能否在 10/20 前完成 
 延遲主因為代工合約與授權合約須同時對接：代工廠要求提前 6
個月下單、授權廠商僅願提前 3 個月，另有包裝規格（一盒 30
片或 60 片）、廠區提貨與保險、 CMO 印製標籤等細節  
 首份合約約 70 多頁，後續合約可望加快；藥證尚未取得，簽約
早晚影響不大  
 出廠價是否會依核價高低彈性調整？  
 已 洽 談 的 五 家均 要 求 比原廠 價 格 低 一 半， 公 司 會提出counter 
offer  
 評估重點為出廠價、對方售價與分潤合計的絕對值，避免低價破
壞行情 
 授權條件與簽約金規模如何考量？  
 因自有藥證並負責供貨，對方銷售量公司皆能掌握；對方要求半
獨家，若要獨家則需更高簽約金  
 對方可能在簽約金與後續里程碑間取捨；公司偏好長線穩健、使
營收最大化，而非追求高簽約金  
 若 BMS 提告，談判與取證是否會延宕？  
 公司已備妥完整非侵權分析，只需證明 non-infringement ，並認
為新劑型案時程不會拖太久  
 若不與原廠合作，是否須等官司結束才能銷售？  
 物質專利 2027/5/21 到期後，夥伴若評估無侵權可自行 at-risk
上市；最差情況約 11M28 與學名藥同時上市  
 若 BMS 不提告或無合作意願，將馬上轉找其他對象；不論與誰
合作，公司希望取證前即有明確授權時程  
 2028 年學名藥進入後價格是否會崩跌？  
 原廠藥價與美國政策較相關；原廠轉向直銷，每顆仍可賣 4-5 美
元，泰合定價以原廠為錨、不與學名藥比

### Page 5

台灣 泰合

十月 01, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

 長效劑型是否可能與原廠產生 訴訟？ 
 長效劑型可涵蓋整個 Apixaban 市場而非僅吞嚥困難族群，公司
認為原廠較可能前來洽談授權而非提告  
 原廠洽談 TAH3311 時亦可能一併考慮 TAH3341

### Page 1

2026.09.30 
陳靖元 C00190@ibfs.com.tw 
7950.TT 盛大資訊 
 數位轉型的即戰力 在手案量能見度高

公司簡介 
盛大資訊股份有限公司成立於 2005 年，為專注於提供大型企業數位轉型與關鍵系統
建置之專業資訊服務商。公司業務模式包含資訊委外、系統整合、客製化軟體開發與
長期系統維運，為客戶提供從擴產衍生的基礎建設到日常營運之一站式整合服務。    
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
未評等 -

➢ 公司以資訊委外及系統整合作為營運主軸 
資訊委外服務為盛大資訊最主要的營收支柱與現金流，佔公司整體營收
約七成至八成。在資訊委外的商業本質上，盛大資訊為大型企業客戶解
決數位轉型高峰期的技術斷層與人力彈性配置痛點，提供具備深度工程
管理實力的技術支援與跨功能專案團隊協作。系統整合營收佔比約
15%~20%， 為公司第二大營收來源 ， 也是切入大型客戶初期基礎建設的
敲門磚。此服務主要聚焦於企業在建廠／資訊環境建置及大型系統架構
翻新階段，提供網路整體架構規劃與建設、機房專業工程、伺服器存儲
設備建置及私有系統虛擬化佈署等統包服務。 
➢ 成長動能多元且訂單能見度高 
當前公司營運動能正迎來四大成長動能：包含半導體全球擴產帶動的舊
系統現代化轉置工程、衛福部「醫療深耕計畫」預算挹注下的醫學中心
多院區骨幹網路整合、金融核心 系統中台化改造，以及跨國電信設備龍
頭之供應商集中化紅利，在手專案合約能見度已達 2028 年。登錄興櫃
將有助於公司獲取指標客戶與超大型專案，推動中長期營收與獲利維持
強勁的成長。  
➢ 企業加速布局 AI 基礎設施，維運需求遞增，帶動營收獲利成長 
財務方面 ，1H26 營收約 3.69 億元， 毛利率約18.3%，稅後淨利 0.12 億
元，EPS1.03 元。雖然過往營收認列旺季多落在下半年，然而隨著公司
接案量能逐年成長，營收規模亦同步放大。展望 2H26 及 2027 年，隨
著半導體客戶放量以及醫療院區系統 設備陸續交貨，再加上資訊委外服
務穩定貢獻，預估營收可望迎來高速成長。 
看好盛大資訊將陸續擴大團隊規模，有利於後續 業務成長，加上在手案
量能見度高以及終端應用組合相對複雜，營收及獲利規模可望維持成長
態勢，另考量公司登錄興櫃後，亦有助於 獲得新的大型客戶合約，再添
成長動能。此外，雖然資金集中在 AI、半導體相關個股，然而看好公司
參與半導體廠自動化改寫專案，有助於後續評價朝區間上緣靠攏。
